# A Useful tools

We state a martingale concentration result, which is a special case of [Pin94, Theorem 3.2] which applies to a more general Banach space.

Theorem 43 (Concentration of martingales in separable Hilbert spaces). Consider a martingale  $Z_{n} \in \mathbb{Z}$  a separable Hilbert space, adapted to  $\mathcal{F}_n$ , such that  $|Z_{n} - Z_{n - 1}| \leq R$  and  $Z_0 = 0$ . Then for any  $t > 0$

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\max  _ {k \leq n} | Z _ {k} | \geq t\right) \leq 2 \inf  _ {\lambda > 0} \exp \left(- \lambda t + \operatorname {e s s - s u p} \sum_ {k = 1} ^ {n} \mathbb {E} \left[ e ^ {\lambda | Z _ {k} - Z _ {k - 1} |} - 1 - \lambda | Z _ {k} - Z _ {k - 1} | \Big | \mathcal {F} _ {k - 1} \right]\right).
$$

In particular, for  $t < nR$ ,

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\max  _ {k \leq n} | Z _ {k} | \geq t\right) \leq 2 \exp \left(- \frac {t ^ {2}}{1 6 n R ^ {2}}\right).
$$

Proof. The first part follows from [Pin94, Theorem 3.2]. The second part follows from the observation that for  $\lambda < 1/(2R)$ ,

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ e ^ {\lambda | Z _ {k} - Z _ {k - 1} |} - 1 - \lambda | Z _ {k} - Z _ {k - 1} | \Big | \mathcal {F} _ {k - 1} \right] <   4 \lambda^ {2} R ^ {2},
$$

and as such we have for  $t < nR$

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\max  _ {k \leq n} | Z _ {k} | \geq t\right) \leq 2 \inf  _ {0 <   \lambda <   1 / (2 R)} \exp (- \lambda t + 4 n \lambda^ {2} R ^ {2}) \leq 2 \exp \left(- \frac {t ^ {2}}{1 6 n R ^ {2}}\right).
$$

![](images/20af9ea2a09b7b841dae773e5fbd50e56696e252d274ea9c24aa6bdd82a8f933.jpg)

Next we state two results for  $\eta$ -independent random variables in separable Hilbert spaces.

Theorem 44 (Concentration of  $\eta$ -independent bounded sum in separable Hilbert spaces). Consider  $n$ $\eta$ -independent random variables  $X_{1},\ldots,X_{n}$  in a separable Hilbert space, where  $\eta \in [0,1]$ . Suppose that  $|X_{i} - \mathbb{E}[X_{i}]|\leq R$  almost surely. Then for  $\delta >2\eta R$ , we have

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\frac {1}{n} \left| \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} X _ {i} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {i} ] \right| \geq \delta\right) \leq 2 \exp \left(- \frac {n \delta^ {2}}{6 4 R ^ {2}}\right).
$$

Proof. Since  $|X_i - \mathbb{E}[X_i]| \leq R$ , the claims are immediate for  $\delta \geq R$ . Let

$$
Z _ {i} = X _ {1} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {1} ] + X _ {2} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {2} | X _ {1} ] + \dots + X _ {i} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {i} | X _ {1}, \ldots , X _ {i - 1} ].
$$

Then  $Z_{i}$  is a martingale adapted to  $\mathcal{F}_i = \sigma (X_1,\ldots ,X_{i - 1})$ . By Theorem 43,

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(| Z _ {n} | \geq t\right) \leq 2 \exp \left(- \frac {t ^ {2}}{1 6 n R ^ {2}}\right),
$$

assuming that  $t < nR$ . Using the  $\eta$ -independence property, we have that for  $\delta \in (2\eta R, (1 + \eta)R)$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {P} \left(\left| \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} X _ {i} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {i} ] \right| \geq \delta n\right) \leq \mathbb {P} \left(| Z _ {n} | \geq \delta n - n \eta R\right) \\ \leq 2 \exp \left(- \frac {(\delta - \eta R) ^ {2} n}{1 6 R ^ {2}}\right) \\ \leq 2 \exp \left(- \delta^ {2} n / \left(6 4 R ^ {2}\right)\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

![](images/027e81e72263323a5acdb9cc13313d66150fab25fa928bd1c84291ddf2e71ea9.jpg)

Theorem 45 (Moments of  $\eta$ -independent heavy-tailed sum in separable Hilbert spaces). Consider  $(X_{1}, X_{2}, \ldots)$  being  $\eta$ -independent random variables in a separable Hilbert space. Suppose that for some constant  $k \geq 1$  (with  $k \leq K$ ), for any  $i \in \mathbb{N}_{>0}$ ,

$$
\sup_{m\geq 1}m^{-k / 2}\mathbb{E}\left[|X_{i}|^{m}\right]^{1 / m}\leq K.
$$

Then for  $m\geq 1$

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ \left| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} X _ {i} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {i} ] \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} \leq K m ^ {1 + k / 2} \max  \left(\eta n ^ {0. 0 1}, n ^ {- 1 / 2}\right).
$$

Proof. It is easy to see that it suffices to prove the claim for  $m \geq 2$ . Let us define  $\mathcal{F}_i = \sigma(X_1, \ldots, X_{i-1})$  and

$$
Z _ {i} = X _ {1} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {1} ] + X _ {2} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {2} | \mathcal {F} _ {2} ] + \dots + X _ {i} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {i} | \mathcal {F} _ {i} ].
$$

Then  $Z_{i}$  is a martingale adapted to  $\mathcal{F}_i$ . Note that for any  $m \geq 1$  and  $B > 0$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} X _ {i} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {i} ] \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} \leq \mathbb {E} [ | Z _ {n} | ^ {m} ] ^ {1 / m} + \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbb {E} [ X _ {i} | \mathcal {F} _ {i} ] - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {i} ] \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} \\ \leq \mathbb {E} \left[\left| Z _ {n} \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} + \mathbb {E} \left[ \right.\left| \right. \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbb {E} \left[ \right. X _ {i} \mathbb {I} _ {\left| X _ {i} \right| \geq B} \left. \right\rvert   \mathcal {F} _ {i} \left. \right] - \mathbb {E} \left[ X _ {i} \mathbb {I} _ {\left| X _ {i} \right| \geq B} \right]\left. \right| ^ {m} \left. \right] ^ {1 / m} + n \eta B \\ \leq \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| Z _ {n} \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} + n ^ {1 - 1 / m} \left(\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| X _ {i} \right| ^ {m} \mathbb {I} _ {\left| X _ {i} \right| \geq B} \right]\right) ^ {1 / m} + n \eta B. \\ \end{array}
$$

By [Pin94, Theorem 4.1], for  $m \geq 2$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} \left[\left| Z _ {n} \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} \leq K m \left(\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbb {E} \left[\left| X _ {i} \right| ^ {m} \right]\right) ^ {1 / m} + K \sqrt {m} \mathbb {E} \left[ \right.\left( \right.\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbb {E} \left[ \right.\left| X _ {i} - \mathbb {E} \left[ X _ {i} \mid \mathcal {F} _ {i} \right]\right| ^ {2} \left. \right\rvert   \mathcal {F} _ {i} \left. \right]\left. \right) ^ {m / 2} \left. \right] ^ {1 / m} \\ \leq K m \left(\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbb {E} \left[ | X _ {i} | ^ {m} \right]\right) ^ {1 / m} + K \sqrt {m} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left(\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbb {E} \left[ | X _ {i} | ^ {2} \mid \mathcal {F} _ {i} \right]\right) ^ {m / 2} \right] ^ {1 / m} \\ \leq K \left(m + \sqrt {m} n ^ {1 / 2 - 1 / m}\right) \left(\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbb {E} \left[ | X _ {i} | ^ {m} \right]\right) ^ {1 / m} \\ \leq K \left(m n ^ {1 / m} + \sqrt {m} n ^ {1 / 2}\right) m ^ {k / 2} \\ \leq K m ^ {1 + k / 2} n ^ {1 / 2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

We also have:

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ | X _ {i} | ^ {m} \mathbb {I} _ {| X _ {i} | \geq B} \right] \leq \mathbb {E} \left[ | X _ {i} | ^ {2 m} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \mathbb {P} \left(| X _ {i} | \geq B\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K ^ {m} m ^ {m k / 2} \exp \left(- K B ^ {2 / k}\right),
$$

since  $|X_i|^{1/k}$  is  $K$ -sub-Gaussian. Therefore,

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ \left| \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} X _ {i} - \mathbb {E} [ X _ {i} ] \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} \leq K m ^ {1 + k / 2} n ^ {1 / 2} + K m ^ {k / 2} \exp \left(- K B ^ {2 / k} / m\right) n + n \eta B.
$$

The claim is satisfied for  $B^{2/k} = cm \log n$  with a suitable constant  $c$ .

# B Remaining proofs for Section 3

# B.1 Proof of Lemma 8

Proof of Lemma 8. Let  $\eta (i) = 2^{L - i}$  and  $\bar{\eta} (i) = 2^{i - 1}$ . Let us define

$$
\llbracket w _ {i} \rrbracket_ {m, t} = \sqrt {\frac {5 0}{m}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} | w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) | ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m}, \quad i = 2, \dots , L.
$$

We prove the following by backward induction, for  $i = 2,\dots,L$  and any  $m\geq 50$

$$
\sqrt{\frac{50}{m}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(s,Z,C_{i}\right)\right|^{m}\right]^{1 / m}\leq K^{3\eta (i - 1) - 2}\left(1 + t^{\eta (i) - 1}\right)\prod_{j = i + 1}^{L}\left(1 + \llbracket w_{j}\rrbracket_{m,0}^{\bar{\eta} (j - i)} + \| |b_{j}\| |_{0}^{\bar{\eta} (j - i)}\right),
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \llbracket w _ {i} \rrbracket_ {m, t} \leq K ^ {3 \eta (i - 1) - 1} \left(1 + \llbracket w _ {i} \rrbracket_ {m, 0}\right) \left(1 + t ^ {\eta (i)}\right) \prod_ {j = i + 1} ^ {L} \left(1 + \llbracket w _ {j} \rrbracket_ {m, 0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i)} + \| | b _ {j} \| | | _ {0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i)}\right), \\ \| b _ {i} \| _ {t} \leq K ^ {3 \eta (i - 1) - 1} \left(1 + \| | b _ {i} \| | _ {0}\right) \left(1 + t ^ {\eta (i)}\right) \prod_ {j = i + 1} ^ {L} \left(1 + \llbracket w _ {j} \rrbracket_ {m, 0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i)} + \| | b _ {j} \| | _ {0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i)}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

for some immaterial constant  $K \geq 1$ , where by standard convention  $\prod_{j=i+1}^{L} = 1$  if  $i = L$ .

Let us start with  $i = L$ . By Assumption 3, for  $\mathcal{P}$ -almost every  $z$ ,

$$
\sup  _ {t \geq 0} \left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {H} (t, z, 1) \right| \leq K.
$$

Consequently, for  $\mathcal{P}$ -almost every  $z$ ,

$$
\max \left(\sup _ {t \geq 0} \sup _ {c _ {L - 1} \in \Omega_ {L - 1}} \left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {w} (t, z, c _ {L - 1}, 1) \right|, \sup _ {t \geq 0} \left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {b} (t, z, 1) \right|\right) \leq K \left(1 + \sup _ {t \geq 0} \left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {H} (t, z, 1) \right|\right) \leq K ^ {2}.
$$

Together with Assumption 1 and the fact  $w_{L}$  and  $b_{L}$  satisfy the MF ODEs, this implies:

$$
\llbracket w _ {L} \rrbracket_ {m, t} \leq \llbracket w _ {L} \rrbracket_ {m, 0} + K ^ {2} t, \quad \left\| \left\| b _ {L} \right\| \right\| _ {t} \leq \left\| \left\| b _ {L} \right\| \right\| _ {0} + K ^ {2} t.
$$

These prove the statement for  $i = L$ .

Next, assuming the statement for  $i + 1$ , we prove the statement for  $i$ , where  $1 < i < L$ . Using Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality, we have from Assumption 3, for  $m \geq 50$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (s, Z, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {m} \right] \\ \leq K^{m}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\mathbb{E}_{C_{i + 1}}\left[ \left(1 + \left|\Delta_{i + 1}^{H}(s,Z,C_{i + 1})\right|\right)\left(1 + \left|w_{i + 1}(s,C_{i},C_{i + 1})\right| + \left|b_{i + 1}(s,C_{i + 1})\right|\right)\right]\right|^{m}\right] \\ \leq K ^ {m} \mathbb {E} \left[ \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i + 1}} \left[ 1 + \sup _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i + 1} ^ {H} (s, Z, C _ {i + 1}) \right| ^ {2} \right] \right. ^ {m / 2} \\ \times \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i + 1}} \left[ 1 + \sup  _ {s \leq t} | w _ {i + 1} (s, C _ {i}, C _ {i + 1}) | ^ {2} + \sup  _ {s \leq t} | b _ {i + 1} (s, C _ {i + 1}) | ^ {2} \right] ^ {m / 2} \Bigg ] \\ \leq K^{m}\left(1 + \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i + 1}^{H}\left(s,Z,C_{i + 1}\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{m / 2}\right) \\ \times \mathbb {E} \left[ 1 + \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i + 1}} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} | w _ {i + 1} (s, C _ {i}, C _ {i + 1}) | ^ {m} \right] + \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} | b _ {i + 1} (s, C _ {i + 1}) | ^ {2} \right] ^ {m / 2} \right] \\ \leq K^{m}\left(1 + \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i + 1}^{H}\left(s,Z,C_{i + 1}\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{m / 2}\right)\left(1 + m^{m / 2}\llbracket w_{i + 1}\rrbracket_{m,t}^{m} + \| |b_{i + 1}\| |_{t}^{m}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies, by the induction hypothesis,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sqrt {\frac {5 0}{m}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (s, Z, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} \\ \leq K \left(1 + \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i + 1}^{H}\left(s,Z,C_{i + 1}\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2}\right) \left(1 + \llbracket w_{i + 1}\rrbracket_{m,t} + \| |b_{i + 1}\| |_{t}\right) \\ \leq K \left[ 1 + K ^ {3 \eta (i) - 2} \left(1 + t ^ {\eta (i + 1) - 1}\right) \prod_ {j = i + 2} ^ {L} \left(1 + \llbracket w _ {j} \rrbracket_ {m, 0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i - 1)} + \| | b _ {j} \| | _ {0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i - 1)}\right) \right] \\ \times \left[ 1 + K ^ {3 \eta (i) - 1} \left(1 + [ [ w _ {i + 1} ] ] _ {m, 0} + | | | b _ {i + 1} | | | _ {0}\right) \left(1 + t ^ {\eta (i + 1)}\right) \prod_ {j = i + 2} ^ {L} \left(1 + [ [ w _ {j} ] ] _ {m, 0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i - 1)} + | | | b _ {j} | | | _ {0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i - 1)}\right) \right] \\ \leq K ^ {3 \eta (i - 1) - 2} \left(1 + t ^ {\eta (i) - 1}\right) \prod_ {j = i + 1} ^ {L} \left(1 + \llbracket w _ {j} \rrbracket_ {m, 0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i)} + \| | b _ {j} \| | _ {0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i)}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

Therefore, by Assumptions 3 and 1, with the fact that  $w_{i}$  satisfies the MF ODEs:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \llbracket w _ {i} \rrbracket_ {m, t} = \sqrt {\frac {5 0}{m}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} \left(0, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) - \int_ {0} ^ {s} \xi_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \Delta_ {i} ^ {w} \left(s ^ {\prime}, Z, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right] d s ^ {\prime} \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} \\ \leq \llbracket w _ {i} \rrbracket_ {m, 0} + \frac {K}{\sqrt {m}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \underset {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} {\operatorname {e s s - s u p}} | \Delta_ {i} ^ {w} (s, Z, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) | ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} t \\ \leq \llbracket w _ {i} \rrbracket_ {m, 0} + K \left(1 + \frac {1}{\sqrt {m}} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (s, Z, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m}\right) t \\ \leq K ^ {3 \eta (i - 1) - 1} \left(1 + \llbracket w _ {i} \rrbracket_ {m, 0}\right) \left(1 + t ^ {\eta (i)}\right) \prod_ {j = i + 1} ^ {L} \left(1 + \llbracket w _ {j} \rrbracket_ {m, 0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i)} + \| | b _ {j} \| | | _ {0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - i)}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

We obtain a similar bound for  $\| |b_i\| _t$ . This completes the backward induction. With the same argument, one can obtain a similar bound for  $i = 1$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sqrt{\frac{50}{m}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{1}^{H}\left(s,Z,C_{1}\right)\right|^{m}\right]^{1 / m}\\ \leq K^{3\eta (0) - 2}\left(1 + t^{\eta (1) - 1}\right)\prod_{j = 2}^{L}\left(1 + \llbracket w_{j}\rrbracket_{m,0}^{\bar{\eta} (j - 1)} + \| |b_{j}\| \|_{0}^{\bar{\eta} (j - 1)}\right), \\ \| w _ {1} \| _ {t} \leq K ^ {3 \eta (0) - 1} \left(1 + \| | w _ {1} \| | _ {0}\right) \left(1 + t ^ {\eta (1)}\right) \prod_ {j = 2} ^ {L} \left(1 + [   [ w _ {j} ]   ] _ {m, 0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - 1)} + \| | b _ {j} \| | _ {0} ^ {\bar {\eta} (j - 1)}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

By taking the supremum on  $m$  or setting  $m = 50$ , these bounds imply the claimed bound on  $\llbracket W\rrbracket_{\psi ,t}$  and  $\| |W|||_t$ . In addition, the bounds on  $\llbracket w_i\rrbracket_{m,t}$  show that  $\sup_{s\leq t}|w_i(s,C_{i - 1},C_i)|$  is  $K_{0}(t)$ -sub-Gaussian for  $2\leq i\leq L$ . Together with the union bound, we then get the claimed probability bound.

# B.2 Proof of Lemma 10

We state the following two useful auxiliary lemmas:

Lemma 46. Consider two collections of  $MF$  parameters  $W^{\prime}, W^{\prime \prime} \in \mathcal{W}_T$ . Under Assumption 2, for any  $t \leq T$  and  $1 \leq i \leq L$ , the following hold:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{X\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|H_{i}\left(X,C_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - H_{i}\left(X,C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2}\leq K^{L}K^{L}_{0}\left(T\right)\left\| W^{\prime} - W^{\prime \prime}\right\|_{t}, \\ \sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess -sup}_{X\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\hat{y}\left(X;W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - \hat{y}\left(X;W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right| \leq K^{L}K_{0}^{L}\left(T\right)\left\| W^{\prime} - W^{\prime \prime}\right\|_{t}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Lemma 47. For a given  $B \geq 0$ , consider two collections of  $MF$  parameters  $W', W'' \in \mathcal{W}_T$  such that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {P} \left(\max  _ {T} ^ {w} \left(W ^ {\prime}\right) \geq K _ {0} (T) B\right) \leq 2 L e ^ {1 - K _ {1} B ^ {2}}, \\ \mathbb {P} \left(\max  _ {T} ^ {w} \left(W ^ {\prime \prime}\right) \geq K _ {0} (T) B\right) \leq 2 L e ^ {1 - K _ {1} B ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Under Assumptions 2 and 3, for any  $t \leq T$  and  $2 \leq i \leq L$ , the following hold:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess -sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta^{w}_{i}\left(Z,C_{i - 1},C_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - \Delta^{w}_{i}\left(Z,C_{i - 1},C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2}\leq D\left(t,W^{\prime},W^{\prime \prime}\right), \\ \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta^{b}_{i}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - \Delta^{b}_{i}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2}\leq D\left(t,W^{\prime},W^{\prime \prime}\right), \\ \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess -sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\bigl|\Delta_{1}^{w}\left(Z,C_{1};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - \Delta_{1}^{w}\left(Z,C_{1};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\bigr|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2}\leq D\left(t,W^{\prime},W^{\prime \prime}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

in which  $D\left(t,W^{\prime},W^{\prime \prime}\right)\stackrel {\mathrm{def}}{=}\left(KK_{0}\left(T\right)\right)^{2L + 2}\left((1 + B)\| W^{\prime} - W^{\prime \prime}\|_{t} + \sqrt{L} e^{-K_{1}B^{2} / 2}\right)$

These lemmas lay the foundation to prove Lemma 10.

Proof of Lemma 10. Let us recall the quantity  $D(t, W', W'')$  from Lemma 47. Let us note a simple identity:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} _ {C} \left[ \left(\int_ {0} ^ {t} f (s, C) d s\right) ^ {2} \right] = \int_ {0} ^ {t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathbb {E} _ {C} [ f (s _ {1}, C) f (s _ {2}, C) ] d s _ {1} d s _ {2} \\ \leq \int_ {0} ^ {t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathbb {E} _ {C} \left[ | f (s _ {1}, C) | ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \mathbb {E} _ {C} \left[ | f (s _ {2}, C) | ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} d s _ {1} d s _ {2} \\ = \left(\int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathbb {E} _ {C} \left[ | f (s, C) | ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} d s\right) ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Now for any  $i\geq 2$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left(\int_ {0} ^ {t} \left| \frac {\partial}{\partial t} F _ {i} ^ {w} (W ^ {\prime}) (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) - \frac {\partial}{\partial t} F _ {i} ^ {w} (W ^ {\prime \prime}) (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| d s\right) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ = \mathbb {E} \left[ \left(\int_ {0} ^ {t} \left| \xi_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (s) \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \Delta_ {i} ^ {w} (Z, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {w} (Z, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right] \right| d s\right) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \stackrel {(a)} {\leq} K \mathbb {E} \left[ \left(\int_ {0} ^ {t} \left| \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \Delta_ {i} ^ {w} (Z, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {w} (Z, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right] \right| d s\right) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \stackrel {(b)} {\leq} K \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \Delta_ {i} ^ {w} (Z, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {w} (Z, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right] \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} d s \\ \stackrel {(c)} {\leq} K \int_ {0} ^ {t} D \left(s, W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right) d s, \\ \end{array}
$$

where  $(a)$  is due to Assumption 1,  $(b)$  is by the aforementioned identity, and  $(c)$  is an application of Lemma 47. Therefore,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| F _ {i} ^ {w} (W ^ {\prime}) (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) - F _ {i} ^ {w} (W ^ {\prime \prime}) (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left(\int_ {0} ^ {s} \left| \frac {\partial}{\partial t} F _ {i} ^ {w} (W ^ {\prime}) (s ^ {\prime}, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) - \frac {\partial}{\partial t} F _ {i} ^ {w} (W ^ {\prime \prime}) (s ^ {\prime}, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| d s ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K \int_ {0} ^ {t} D (s, W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}) d s. \\ \end{array}
$$

One can show the same bound for  $F_{i}^{b}$  and  $F_{1}^{w}$ . This completes the proof.

![](images/cfbceae6fdb8c14fc4d3b95cdc150f049555b4eea905755c0ade0f3c85c2be16.jpg)

Lemmas 46 and 47 are in fact special cases of the following lemmas.

Lemma 48. Consider two collections of  $MF$  parameters  $W^{\prime}, W^{\prime \prime} \in \mathcal{W}_T$ . Suppose that we define  $\tilde{C}_1, \dots, \tilde{C}_L$  independent random variables on  $\Omega_1, \dots, \Omega_L$ , such that  $\tilde{C}_i$  is independent of  $C_1, \dots, C_{i-1}, C_{i+1}, \dots, C_L$ , and that there exists some  $K_*(T) \geq K_0(T)$  such that all following quantities are upper-bounded by  $K_*(T)$  for all  $t \leq T$  and for  $W = W^{\prime}$  or  $W = W^{\prime \prime}$ :

$$
\max _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0}, \max _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0}, \max _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} | w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) | ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0}, \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| b _ {i} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0}, \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} | b _ {i} (s, C _ {i}) | ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0}, \\ \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {1} (s, \tilde {C} _ {1}) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0}, \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} | w _ {1} (s, C _ {1}) | ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Under Assumption 2, for any  $t \leq T$  and  $1 \leq i \leq L$ , we have:

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {X \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| H _ {i} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - H _ {i} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \leq K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L} (T)   \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right),
$$

and the same holds if we replace  $\tilde{C}_i$  with  $C_i$  in the left-hand side of the above. Here we have defined the metrics:

$$
\tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right) = \max  \left(\max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(w _ {i} ^ {\prime}, w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime}\right), \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(b _ {i} ^ {\prime}, b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime}\right), \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(w _ {1} ^ {\prime}, w _ {1} ^ {\prime \prime}\right)\right),
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(w _ {i} ^ {\prime}, w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime}\right) = \max  \left(\mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} \left(s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}\right) - w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} \left(s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}, \right. \\ \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) - w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) - w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) - w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(b _ {i} ^ {\prime}, b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime}\right) = \max  \left(\mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) - b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}, \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i}) - b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}\right) \\ \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(w _ {1} ^ {\prime}, w _ {1} ^ {\prime \prime}\right) = \max  \left(\mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {1} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {1}) - w _ {1} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {1}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}, \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {1} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {1}) - w _ {1} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {1}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

(Note that the random variables  $\tilde{C}_i$  are general, and may be chosen to be equal to  $C_i$ . The space  $\mathcal{W}_T$  which contains  $W'$  and  $W''$  is defined with respect to the random variables  $C_1, \ldots, C_L$ .)

Lemma 49. Consider two collections of  $MF$  parameters  $W^{\prime}, W^{\prime \prime} \in \mathcal{W}_T$ . Suppose we define the random variables  $\tilde{C}_1, \dots, \tilde{C}_L$ , the bounding constant  $K_*(T)$  and the metric  $\tilde{d}_t(W^{\prime}, W^{\prime \prime})$  as given in the statement of Lemma 48. Further assume that for some non-negative function  $\Xi$  and some  $B \geq 0$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {P} \left(\widetilde {\max } _ {T} ^ {w} \left(W ^ {\prime}\right) \geq K _ {*} (T) B\right) \leq \Xi (B), \\ \mathbb {P} \left(\widetilde {\operatorname {m a x}} _ {T} ^ {w} \left(W ^ {\prime \prime}\right) \geq K _ {*} (T) B\right) \leq \Xi (B), \\ \end{array}
$$

in which we define

$$
\widetilde {\operatorname {m a x}} _ {t} ^ {w} \left(W ^ {\prime}\right) = \max _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \sup _ {s \leq t} \max \left(\left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} \left(s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}\right) \right|, \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} \left(s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right|, \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} \left(s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}\right) \right|, \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} \left(s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right|\right).
$$

Under Assumptions 2 and 3, for any  $t \leq T$  and  $2 \leq i \leq L$ , we have:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\Big|\Delta_{i}^{w}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i - 1},\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime}(s)\right) - \Delta_{i}^{w}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i - 1},\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime \prime}(s)\right)\Big|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2}\leq \tilde{D}\left(t,W^{\prime},W^{\prime \prime}\right), \\ \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i}^{b}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - \Delta_{i}^{b}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2}\leq \tilde{D}\left(t,W^{\prime},W^{\prime \prime}\right), \\ \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\Big|\Delta_{1}^{w}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{1};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - \Delta_{1}^{w}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{1};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\Big|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2}\leq \tilde{D}\left(t,W^{\prime},W^{\prime \prime}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

and the same holds if we replace  $\tilde{C}_i$  or  $\tilde{C}_{i - 1}$  with  $C_i$  or  $C_{i - 1}$  respectively in the left-hand side of each line above. Here

$$
\tilde {D} (t, W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}) \stackrel {\mathrm {d e f}} {=} (K K _ {*} (T)) ^ {3 L + 2} ((1 + B) \tilde {d} _ {t} (W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}) + \sqrt {\Xi (B)}).
$$

Next we prove each of the remaining lemmas.

# B.3 Proof of Lemmas 46 and 48

Proof of Lemma 46. The first bound is a direct corollary of Lemma 48 by setting  $\tilde{C}_i = C_i$  for all  $i\in [L]$ . In addition, by Assumption 2,

$$
\sup _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {X \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \hat {y} (X; W ^ {\prime} (s)) - \hat {y} (X; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right| \leq K D _ {L} (t) \leq K ^ {L} K _ {0} ^ {L} (T) \left\| W ^ {\prime} - W ^ {\prime \prime} \right\| _ {t},
$$

completing the proof.

![](images/d3f2369d71a5ed72d93b314f636ef61f0f98e3244e8471057ba3ef1f294ef09b.jpg)

Proof of Lemma 48. Let us denote

$$
D_{i}\left(t\right) = \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{X\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|H_{i}\left(X,C_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - H_{i}\left(X,C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2},
$$

$$
\tilde {D} _ {i} (t) = \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} \underset {X \sim \mathcal {P}} {\operatorname {e s s - s u p}} \left| H _ {i} (X, \tilde {C} _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) - H _ {i} (X, \tilde {C} _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}.
$$

By Assumption 2, for  $\tilde{D}_1(t)$ :

$$
\tilde {D} _ {1} (t) \leq K \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {1} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {1}) - w _ {1} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {1}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \leq K \tilde {d} _ {t} (W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}).
$$

The same bound holds for  $D_{1}(t)$ . Next let us consider  $\tilde{D}_i(t)$ . By Assumption 2, using Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality, we obtain:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {D} _ {i} (t) \leq K \mathbb {E} \left[ \right. \sup  _ {s \leq t} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[\left(1 + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right|\right)\right. \\ \left. \times \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {X \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| H _ {i - 1} \left(X, C _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - H _ {i - 1} \left(X, C _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| \right] ^ {2} \biggr ] ^ {1 / 2} \\ + K \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) - w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) - b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| \right] ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ 1 + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} \right] D _ {i - 1} ^ {2} (t) \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ + K \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) - w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i - 1}, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) - b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| \right] ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \le K K _ {*} (T) D _ {i - 1} (t) + K \tilde {d} _ {t} (W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}). \\ \end{array}
$$

We have the same bound for  $D_{i}(t)$ . Hence,

$$
\left. \max  \left(D _ {i} (t), \tilde {D} _ {i} (t)\right) \leq K K _ {*} (T) \max  \left(D _ {i - 1} (t), \tilde {D} _ {i - 1} (t)\right) + K \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right). \right.
$$

This in particular implies

$$
\max _ {1 \leq i \leq L} \max \left(D _ {i} (t), \tilde {D} _ {i} (t)\right) \leq K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L} (T) \tilde {d} _ {t} (W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}),
$$

which proves the statement.

![](images/3d34cb5087537e4ac31f1965e06c594aaf6df456aa80aa5779aba7bc7560c2a7.jpg)

# B.4 Proof of Lemmas 47 and 49

Proof of Lemma 47. This is a special case of Lemma 49 with  $\tilde{C}_i = C_i$  for all  $i\in [L]$ ,  $K_{*}(T) = K_{0}(T)$  and  $\Xi (B) = 2Le^{1 - K_1B^2}$ .

Proof of Lemma 49. First of all, by Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality, we have from Assumption 3,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\Big|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right)\Big|^{50}\right]^{1 / 50} \\ \leq K \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \underset {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} {\operatorname {e s s - s u p}} \left| \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i + 1}} \left[ \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i + 1} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i + 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) \right|\right) \left(1 + \left| w _ {i + 1} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}, C _ {i + 1}) \right| + \left| b _ {i + 1} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i + 1}) \right|\right) \right] \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0} \\ \leq K \mathbb {E} \left[ \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i + 1}} \left[ 1 + \sup  _ {s \leq t} \operatorname {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i + 1} ^ {H} \left(Z, C _ {i + 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {2 5} \right. \\ \times \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i + 1}} \left[ 1 + \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i + 1} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}, C _ {i + 1}) \right| ^ {2} + \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| b _ {i + 1} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i + 1}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {2 5} \bigg ] ^ {1 / 5 0} \\ \leq K \left(1 + \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \operatorname {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i + 1} ^ {H} \left(Z, C _ {i + 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}\right) \\ \times \left(1 + \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i + 1} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}, C _ {i + 1}) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0} + \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| b _ {i + 1} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i + 1}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}\right) \\ \leq K K _ {*} \left(T\right) \left(1 + \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i + 1} ^ {H} \left(Z, C _ {i + 1}; W ^ {\prime} \left(s\right)\right) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

We have similarly,

$$
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\bigl|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right)\bigr|^{50}\right]^{1 / 50}\\ \leq KK_{*}\left(T\right)\left(1 + \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\bigl|\Delta_{i + 1}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i + 1};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right)\bigr|^{50}\right]^{1 / 50}\right).
$$

Therefore, for any  $i\in [L]$

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \operatorname {e s s} - \sup  _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0}, \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \operatorname {e s s} - \sup  _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, \tilde {C} _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0} \leq K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L} (T). \tag {18}
$$

The same bound holds for  $W''$ . With this, let us proceed with two steps.

Step 1. For brevity, let us define

$$
D _ {i} ^ {H} (t) = \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} \underset {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} {\operatorname {e s s - s u p}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2},
$$

$$
\tilde {D} _ {i} ^ {H} (t) = \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} \underset {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} {\operatorname {e s s - s u p}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, \tilde {C} _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, \tilde {C} _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}.
$$

We first have from Assumption 3 and Lemma 48:

$$
\begin{array}{l} D_{L}^{H}\left(t\right) = \tilde{D}_{L}^{H}\left(t\right)\leq K\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess -sup}_{X\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|H_{L}\left(X,1;W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - H_{L}\left(X,1;W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right| \\ + K \sup  _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {X \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \hat {y} \left(X; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - \hat {y} \left(X; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| \\ \leq K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L} (T) \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

Next we consider  $\tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H}$  and  $D_{i - 1}^{H}$  for  $i\geq 2$  . By Assumption 3:

$$
\tilde {D} _ {i - 1} ^ {H} (t) \leq K (\tilde {D} _ {i - 1} ^ {H, 1} (t) + \tilde {D} _ {i - 1} ^ {H, 2} (t) + \tilde {D} _ {i - 1} ^ {H, 3} (t) + \tilde {D} _ {i - 1} ^ {H, 4} (t) + \tilde {D} _ {i - 1} ^ {H, 5} (t)),
$$

in which

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H,1}(t) = \mathbb{E}_{\tilde{C}_{i - 1}}\bigg[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\bigg[\Big(1 + \Big|w_{i}^{\prime}\Big(s,\tilde{C}_{i - 1},C_{i}\Big)\Big| + \Big|w_{i}^{\prime \prime}\Big(s,\tilde{C}_{i - 1},C_{i}\Big)\Big| + \Big|b_{i}^{\prime}\big(s,C_{i}\big)\Big| + \Big|b_{i}^{\prime \prime}\big(s,C_{i}\big)\Big|\bigg) \\ \left. \times \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (z, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (z, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right| \right] ^ {2} \biggr ] ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H,2}\left(t\right) = \mathbb{E}_{\tilde{C}_{i - 1}}\bigg[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\bigg[\left(1 + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right| + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|\right) \\ \times \left(\left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) - w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i}) - b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right|\right) \biggr ] ^ {2} \biggr ] ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}^{H,3}_{i - 1}(t) = \mathbb{E}_{\tilde{C}_{i - 1}}\bigg[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\Big[\big(1 + \big|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime}(s)\right)\big| + \big|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}(s)\right)\big|\big) \\ \times \left(1 + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right| + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right|\right) \\ \left. \times \left| H _ {i} \left(X, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - H _ {i} \left(X, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| \right] ^ {2} \bigg ] ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}^{H,4}_{i - 1}(t) = \mathbb{E}_{\tilde{C}_{i - 1}}\bigg[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\bigg[  \big(1 + \big|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime}(s)\right)\big| + \big|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}(s)\right)\big|\big) \\ \left. \times \left(1 + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right| + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right|\right) \right] ^ {2} \\ \times \left. \left| H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}^{H,5}_{i - 1}(t) = \mathbb{E}_{\tilde{C}_{i - 1}}\bigg[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\Big[\big(1 + \big|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime}(s)\right)\big| + \big|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}(s)\right)\big|\big) \\ \left. \times \left(\left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right|\right) \right] ^ {2} \\ \left. \times \left| H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

We bound each term. For  $\tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H,1}$ , we use Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality to obtain:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H,1}(t)\leq K\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{C}_{i - 1}}\left[ \right.\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[\left(1 + \left|w_{i}^{\prime}\left(s,\tilde{C}_{i - 1},C_{i}\right)\right|^{2} + \left|w_{i}^{\prime \prime}\left(s,\tilde{C}_{i - 1},C_{i}\right)\right|^{2}\right.\right. \\ \left. \left. + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2}\right) \left| D _ {i} ^ {H} (t) \right| ^ {2} \right] \Bigg ] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K K _ {*} (T) D _ {i} ^ {H} (t). \\ \end{array}
$$

Similarly, using Eq. (18),

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {D} _ {i - 1} ^ {H, 2} (t) \leq K \sup _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ 1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \tilde {d} _ {t} (W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}) \\ \leq K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L} (T) \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

To bound  $\tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H,3}$ , we use Lemma 48 and Eq. (18):

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H,3}\left(t\right)\leq K\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{C}_{i - 1}}\bigg[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[1 + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{4} + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{4}\right]^{1 / 2} \\ \times \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ 1 + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {4} + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {4} + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {4} + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {4} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \times \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ \left| H _ {i} (X, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) - H _ {i} (X, C _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right| ^ {2} \right] \Bigg ] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K ^ {2 L + 2} K _ {*} ^ {2 L + 2} (T) \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

and similarly, for  $\tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H,4}$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H,4}\left(t\right)\leq K\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{C}_{i - 1}}\bigg[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[1 + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2} + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right] \\ \times \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ 1 + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} \right] \\ \times \left. \left| H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K ^ {2 L + 2} K _ {*} ^ {2 L + 2} (T) \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

The treatment of  $\tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H,5}$  requires more care. Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality and Eq. (18) give us:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}_{i - 1}^{H,5}\left(t\right)\leq K\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{C}_{i - 1}}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\mathbb{E}_{C_{i}}\left[1 + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2} + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]\right. \\ \times \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} \right] \\ \times \left| H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| ^ {2} \biggr ] ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L} (T) \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left(\left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2}\right) \right. \\ \left. \times \operatorname {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Recall our assumption:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {P} \left(\widetilde {\operatorname* {m a x}} _ {T} ^ {w} \left(W ^ {\prime}\right) \geq K _ {*} (T) B\right) \leq \Xi (B), \\ \mathbb {P} \left(\widehat {\operatorname {m a x}} _ {T} ^ {w} \left(W ^ {\prime \prime}\right) \geq K _ {*} (T) B\right) \leq \Xi (B). \\ \end{array}
$$

We also have from Assumption 2:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\Bigl|H_{i - 1}\left(X,\tilde{C}_{i - 1};W^{\prime}(s)\right)\Bigr|^{8}\right]^{1 / 8} \\ \leq K \left(1 + \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i - 1} ^ {\prime} (s, C _ {i - 2}, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}) \right| ^ {8} \right] ^ {1 / 8} + \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| b _ {i - 1} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}) \right| ^ {8} \right] ^ {1 / 8}\right) \\ \leq K K _ {*} (T), \\ \end{array}
$$

and similarly,

$$
\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|H_{i - 1}\left(X,\tilde{C}_{i - 1};W^{\prime \prime}(s)\right)\right|^{8}\right]^{1 / 8}\leq KK_{*}\left(T\right).
$$

As such, denoting the event

$$
E = \left\{\sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} \left(s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right| \geq K _ {*} (T)   B, \quad \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} \left(s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right| \geq K _ {*} (T)   B \right\},
$$

weobtainfromLemma48:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {D} _ {i - 1} ^ {H, 5} (t) \leq K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L} (T) \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left(\left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2}\right) \right. \\ \times \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| ^ {2} (\mathbb {I} (\neg E) + \mathbb {I} (E)) \Bigg ] ^ {1 / 2}. \\ \leq K^{L}K_{*}^{L + 1}\left(T\right)B\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|H_{i - 1}\left(X,\tilde{C}_{i - 1};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - H_{i - 1}\left(X,\tilde{C}_{i - 1};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2} \\ + K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L} (T) \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {8} + \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| w _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {8} \right] ^ {1 / 8} \\ \times \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess -sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left(\left|H_{i - 1}\left(X,\tilde{C}_{i - 1};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{8} + \left|H_{i - 1}\left(X,\tilde{C}_{i - 1};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{8}\right)\right]^{1 / 8}\mathbb{P}\left(E\right)^{1 / 2} \\ \leq K ^ {2 L + 2} K _ {*} ^ {2 L + 2} (T) B \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right) + K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L + 2} (T) \sqrt {\Xi (B)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Putting all the bounds together:

$$
\tilde {D} _ {i - 1} ^ {H} (t) \leq K K _ {*} (T) D _ {i} ^ {H} (t) + (K K _ {*} (T)) ^ {2 L + 2} (1 + B) \tilde {d} _ {t} (W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}) + K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L + 2} (T) \sqrt {\Xi (B)}.
$$

Similarly,

$$
D _ {i - 1} ^ {H} (t) \leq K K _ {*} (T) D _ {i} ^ {H} (t) + (K K _ {*} (T)) ^ {2 L + 2} (1 + B) \tilde {d} _ {t} (W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}) + K ^ {L} K _ {*} ^ {L + 2} (T) \sqrt {\Xi (B)}.
$$

Together with the bound on  $D_L^H$  and  $\tilde{D}_L^H$ , we thus obtain:

$$
\max  _ {1 \leq i \leq L} \max  \left(\tilde {D} _ {i} ^ {H} (t), D _ {i} ^ {H} (t)\right) \leq \left(K K _ {*} (T)\right) ^ {3 L + 2} \left((1 + B) \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right) + \sqrt {\Xi (B)}\right). \tag {19}
$$

This completes the first step.

Step 2. We now prove the main claims of the lemma. For brevity, for  $i \geq 2$ , let us denote

$$
\tilde{D}_{i}^{w}\left(t\right) = \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i}^{w}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i - 1},\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - \Delta_{i}^{w}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i - 1},\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2},
$$

$$
\tilde{D}_{i}^{b}\left(t\right) = \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i}^{b}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - \Delta_{i}^{b}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2},
$$

$$
\tilde{D}_{1}^{w}\left(t\right) = \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{1}^{w}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{1};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - \Delta_{1}^{w}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{1};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2}.
$$

By Assumption 2,

$$
\tilde {D} _ {i} ^ {w} (t) \leq K \left(\tilde {D} _ {i} ^ {w, 1} (t) + \tilde {D} _ {i} ^ {w, 2} (t)\right),
$$

in which

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}_{i}^{w,1}\left(t\right) = \mathbb{E}\bigg[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left(1 + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2} + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right) \\ \left. \times \left(\left| H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime} (s)\right) - H _ {i - 1} \left(X, \tilde {C} _ {i - 1}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)\right) \right| ^ {2}\right) \right] ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}_{i}^{w,2}\left(t\right) = \mathbb{E}\bigg[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\bigg(\left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right) - \Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2} + \left|w_{i}^{\prime}\left(s,\tilde{C}_{i - 1},\tilde{C}_{i}\right) - w_{i}^{\prime \prime}\left(s,\tilde{C}_{i - 1},\tilde{C}_{i}\right)\right|^{2}\bigg] \\ \left. + \left| b _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) - b _ {i} ^ {\prime \prime} (s, \tilde {C} _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| H _ {i} (X, \tilde {C} _ {i}; W ^ {\prime} (s)) - H _ {i} (X, \tilde {C} _ {i}; W ^ {\prime \prime} (s)) \right| ^ {2}\right) \biggr ] ^ {1 / 2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

We bound  $\tilde{D}_i^{w,1}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde{D}_{i}^{w,1}\left(t\right)\leq \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left(1 + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2} + \left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,\tilde{C}_{i};W^{\prime \prime}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right)\right]^{1 / 2} \\ \times \mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|H_{i - 1}\left(X,\tilde{C}_{i - 1};W^{\prime}(s)\right) - H_{i - 1}\left(X,\tilde{C}_{i - 1};W^{\prime \prime}(s)\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2} \\ \leq K ^ {2 L} K _ {*} ^ {2 L} \left(T\right) \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where we have used Eq. (18) and Lemma 48. We also have the following bound on  $\tilde{D}_i^{w,2}$  from Lemma 48 and Eq. (19):

$$
\tilde {D} _ {i} ^ {w, 2} (t) \leq \left(K K _ {*} (T)\right) ^ {3 L + 2} \left(\left(1 + B\right) \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right) + \sqrt {\Xi (B)}\right),
$$

which therefore leads to

$$
\tilde {D} _ {i} ^ {w} (t) \leq \left(K K _ {*} (T)\right) ^ {3 L + 2} \left(\left(1 + B\right) \tilde {d} _ {t} \left(W ^ {\prime}, W ^ {\prime \prime}\right) + \sqrt {\Xi (B)}\right).
$$

The same bound similarly applies to  $\tilde{D}_i^b (t)$  and  $\tilde{D}_1^w (t)$ .

# C Remaining proofs for Section 4

# C.1 Proofs of Propositions 22, 23 and 24

Before delving into the proofs, we introduce some auxiliary results. We first present a useful concentration result. In fact, the tail bound can be improved using the argument in [FV18], but the following simpler version is sufficient for our purposes.

Lemma 50. Consider an integer  $n \geq 2$ ; let  $(c_1, c_2, \ldots, c_n)$  be  $\eta$ -independent for  $\eta \in [0,1/2]$  and let  $x$  be another independent random variable. Let  $\mathbb{E}_x$  and  $\mathbb{E}_c$  denote the expectations w.r.t.  $x$  only and  $\{c_i\}_{i \in [n]}$  only, respectively. Consider a collection of mappings  $\{f_i\}_{i \in [n]}$ , which map to the same separable Hilbert space. Let  $f_i(x) = \mathbb{E}_c[f_i(c_i, x)]$ . Assume that  $|f_i(c_i, x) - f_i(x)| \leq R$  for almost every  $x$  and  $c_i$ , then for any  $\delta > 2\eta R$ ,

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\mathbb {E} _ {x} \left[ \left| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} f _ {i} (c _ {i}, x) - f _ {i} (x) \right| \right] \geq \delta\right) \leq \frac {4 R}{\delta} \exp \left(- \frac {n \delta^ {2}}{5 1 2 R ^ {2}}\right).
$$

Proof. For brevity, let us define

$$
Z _ {n} (x) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(f _ {i} \left(c _ {i}, x\right) - f _ {i} (x)\right).
$$

By Theorem 44, for  $\delta >2\eta R$

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(| Z _ {n} (x) | \geq n \delta | x\right) \leq 2 \exp \left(- n \delta^ {2} / \left(6 4 R ^ {2}\right)\right),
$$

and therefore,

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\left| Z _ {n} (x) \right| \geq n \delta\right) \leq 2 \exp \left(- n \delta^ {2} / \left(6 4 R ^ {2}\right)\right),
$$

since the right-hand side is uniform in  $x$ . Next note that, w.r.t. the randomness of  $x$  only,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} _ {x} \left[ | Z _ {n} (x) | \right] = \mathbb {E} _ {x} \left[ | Z _ {n} (x) | \mathbb {I} \left(| Z _ {n} (x) | \geq n \delta / 2\right) \right] + \mathbb {E} _ {x} \left[ | Z _ {n} (x) | \mathbb {I} \left(| Z _ {n} (x) | <   n \delta / 2\right) \right] \\ \leq \mathbb {E} _ {x} \left[ | Z _ {n} (x) | \mathbb {I} \left(| Z _ {n} (x) | \geq n \delta / 2\right) \right] + n \delta / 2. \\ \end{array}
$$

As such, by Markov's inequality and Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {P} \left(\mathbb {E} _ {x} \left[ \left| Z _ {n} (x) \right| \right] \geq n \delta\right) \leq \mathbb {P} \left(\mathbb {E} _ {x} \left[ \left| Z _ {n} (x) \right| \mathbb {I} \left(\left| Z _ {n} (x) \right| \geq n \delta / 2\right) \right] \geq n \delta / 2\right) \\ \leq \frac {2}{n \delta} \mathbb {E} \left[ | Z _ {n} (x) | \mathbb {I} \left(| Z _ {n} (x) | \geq n \delta / 2\right) \right] \\ \leq \frac {2}{n \delta} \mathbb {E} \left[ | Z _ {n} (x) | ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \mathbb {P} \left(| Z _ {n} (x) | \geq n \delta / 2\right) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq \frac {4}{n \delta} \mathbb {E} \left[ | Z _ {n} (x) | ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \exp \left(- \frac {n \delta^ {2}}{5 1 2 R ^ {2}}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

Notice that since  $c_{1},\ldots ,c_{n}$  are  $\eta$  -independent and  $f_{i}(x) = \mathbb{E}_{c}[f_{i}(c_{i},x)]$

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ | Z _ {n} (x) | ^ {2} \right] \leq n R ^ {2} + \eta n ^ {2} R ^ {2}.
$$

We thus get:

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\mathbb {E} _ {x} \left[ | Z _ {n} (x) | \right] \geq n \delta\right) \leq \frac {4 \sqrt {1 + \eta n} R}{\sqrt {n} \delta} \exp \left(- \frac {n \delta^ {2}}{5 1 2 R ^ {2}}\right) \leq \frac {4 R}{\delta} \exp \left(- \frac {n \delta^ {2}}{5 1 2 R ^ {2}}\right).
$$

This proves the claim.

The next useful result concerns with the sampling at initialization.

Lemma 51. Under Assumption 5, following the coupling procedure, we have for any  $\delta >0$  and  $B > 0$ , with probability at least  $1 - KLn_{\mathrm{max}}\exp \left(-K\left(\delta \wedge \delta^{1 / 26}\right)n_{\mathrm{min}}^{1 / 52}\right)$ , the following hold:

moment bounds:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \tilde {W} \| _ {0} = \| \mathbf {W} \| _ {0} \leq \| W \| _ {0} + \delta^ {1 / 5 0}, \\ \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ \left| w _ {i} ^ {0} (C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {5 0} \right]\right) ^ {1 / 5 0} \leq \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| w _ {i} ^ {0} (C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0} + \delta^ {1 / 5 0}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L, \\ \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ \left| w _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {5 0} \right]\right) ^ {1 / 5 0} \leq \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| w _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0} + \delta^ {1 / 5 0}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L, \\ \end{array}
$$

- excess bounds:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {I} \left(\left| w _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right| \geq B\right) - \mathbb {P} \left(\left| w _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right| \geq B\right) \right| \leq \delta , \quad 2 \leq i \leq L, \\ \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {I} \left(\left| b _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right| \geq B\right) - \mathbb {P} \left(\left| b _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i}\right) \right| \geq B\right) \right| \leq \delta , \quad 2 \leq i \leq L. \\ \end{array}
$$

Here  $n_{\mathrm{max}} = \max (n_1,\dots,n_L)$  and  $n_{\mathrm{min}} = \min (n_1,\dots,n_{L - 1})$ .

Proof. We treat the bounds separately.

The moment bounds. We recall that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \| \tilde {W} \| _ {0} = \max  \left( \right.\max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| w _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right)\right| ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}, \\ \max _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| b _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right| ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}, \left(\frac {1}{n _ {1}} \sum_ {j _ {1} = 1} ^ {n _ {1}} \left| w _ {1} ^ {0} \left(C _ {1} \left(j _ {1}\right)\right) \right| ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Let us first prove the following:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {P} \left(Z ^ {(1)} \geq \delta\right) \leq e \cdot \exp \left(- K \delta^ {1 / 2 6} n _ {1} ^ {1 / 5 2}\right), \\ Z ^ {(1)} = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {1}} \sum_ {j _ {1} = 1} ^ {n _ {1}} \left| w _ {1} ^ {0} (C _ {1} (j _ {1})) \right| ^ {5 0} - \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| w _ {1} ^ {0} (C _ {1}) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] \right|. \\ \end{array}
$$

Indeed we note that for any  $m \geq 1$ ,  $\mathbb{E}\left[\left|w_1^0(C_1)\right|^{50m}\right]^{1/m} \leq Km^{25}$ . As such, by Theorem 45,

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ \left| Z ^ {(1)} \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} \leq K m ^ {2 6} n _ {1} ^ {- 1 / 2}.
$$

This implies  $\left|Z^{(1)}\right|^{1/52}$  is  $Kn_1^{-1/104}$ -sub-Gaussian, from which the claim follows. Using the same argument, we get

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {P} \left(Z ^ {(L)} \geq \delta\right) \leq e \cdot \exp \left(- K \delta^ {1 / 2 6} n _ {L - 1} ^ {1 / 5 2}\right), \\ Z ^ {(L)} = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {L - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {L - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {L - 1}} \left| w _ {L} ^ {0} \left(C _ {L - 1} \left(j _ {L - 1}\right), 1\right) \right| ^ {5 0} - \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| w _ {L} ^ {0} \left(C _ {L - 1}, 1\right) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] \right|, \\ \end{array}
$$

as well as that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {P} \left(A ^ {(i)} \geq \delta\right) \leq e \cdot \exp \left(- K \delta^ {1 / 2 6} n _ {i} ^ {1 / 5 2}\right), \\ A ^ {(i)} = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| b _ {i} ^ {0} (C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {5 0} - \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| b _ {i} ^ {0} (C _ {i}) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] \right|, \\ \end{array}
$$

for  $2 \leq i \leq L - 1$ . In addition, since  $\Omega_L = \{1\}$  and  $n_L = 1$ , it is obvious that  $\left|b_L^0 (C_L(j_L))\right| = \left|b_L^0 (C_L)\right|$ . Next for  $2 \leq i \leq L - 1$ , without loss of generality, suppose  $n_i \geq n_{i-1}$ . Let us prove the following:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {P} \left(Z ^ {(i)} \geq \delta\right) \leq e n _ {i} \cdot \exp \left(- K \delta^ {1 / 2 6} n _ {i - 1} ^ {1 / 5 2}\right), \\ Z ^ {(i)} = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| w _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right| ^ {5 0} - \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| w _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] \right|. \\ \end{array}
$$

For fixed  $j_{i}\in [n_{i}]$  , let us first consider

$$
\tilde {C} _ {j _ {i}} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right) = \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(\left(j _ {i - 1} + j _ {i}\right) \mod n _ {i}\right)\right).
$$

For any 1-bounded function  $f$ , due to independence between  $C_i(j_i)$ 's and  $C_{i-1}(j_{i-1})$ 's and Assumption 4, we have:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \mathbb {E} \left[ f \left(\tilde {C} _ {j _ {i}} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right)\right) \right| \left\{\tilde {C} _ {j _ {i}} \left(j _ {i - 1} ^ {\prime}\right) \right\} _ {j _ {i - 1} ^ {\prime} \neq j _ {i - 1}}, C _ {i} \left(\left(j _ {i - 1} + j _ {i}\right) \mod n _ {i}\right) \right] \\ - \mathbb {E} \left[ f \left(\tilde {C} _ {j _ {i}} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right)\right) \mid C _ {i} \left(\left(j _ {i - 1} + j _ {i}\right) \mod n _ {i}\right) \right] \mid \leq \eta_ {i - 1}, \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies

$$
\left| \mathbb {E} \left[ f \left(\tilde {C} _ {j _ {i}} (j _ {i - 1})\right) \middle | \left\{\tilde {C} _ {j _ {i}} (j _ {i - 1} ^ {\prime}) \right\} _ {j _ {i - 1} ^ {\prime} \neq j _ {i - 1}} \right] - \mathbb {E} \left[ f \left(\tilde {C} _ {j _ {i}} (j _ {i - 1})\right) \right] \right| \leq \eta_ {i - 1}.
$$

That is,  $\left\{\tilde{C}_{j_i}(j_{i-1})\right\}_{j_{i-1} \in [n_{i-1}]}$  is  $\eta_{i-1}$ -independent. Hence by the same argument, by letting

$$
Z _ {j _ {i}} ^ {(i)} = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left| w _ {i} ^ {0} (\tilde {C} _ {j _ {i}} (j _ {i - 1})) \right| ^ {5 0} - \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| w _ {i} ^ {0} (\tilde {C} _ {j _ {i}} (j _ {i - 1})) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] \right|,
$$

we have:

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(Z _ {j _ {i}} ^ {(i)} \geq \delta\right) \leq e \cdot \exp \left(- K \delta^ {1 / 2 6} n _ {i - 1} ^ {1 / 5 2}\right).
$$

By the union bound,

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(Z ^ {(i)} \geq \delta\right) \leq \mathbb {P} \left(\max  _ {j _ {i} \leq n _ {i}} Z _ {j _ {i}} ^ {(i)} \geq \delta\right) \leq e n _ {i} \cdot \exp \left(- K \delta^ {1 / 2 6} n _ {i - 1} ^ {1 / 5 2}\right),
$$

which is the desired claim.

Upon an application of the union bound, these probability bounds imply the bound on the probability of the event  $\| \tilde{W} \|_0 = \| \mathbf{W} \|_0 \leq \| W \|_0 + \delta^{1/50}$ . The rest of the bounds are similarly proven.

The excess bounds. Without loss of generality, assume  $n_i \geq n_{i-1}$  for  $2 \leq i \leq L-1$ . Let us denote

$$
D _ {j _ {i}} ^ {(i)} = \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {I} \left(\left| w _ {i} ^ {0} \left(\tilde {C} _ {j _ {i}} (j _ {i - 1})\right) \right| \geq B\right) - \mathbb {P} \left(\left| w _ {i} ^ {0} (C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| \geq B\right).
$$

Recall previously that  $\left\{\tilde{C}_{j_i}(j_{i-1})\right\}_{j_{i-1} \in [n_{i-1}]}$  is  $\eta_{i-1}$ -independent. As such, by Theorem 45,

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ \left| D _ {j _ {i}} ^ {(i)} \right| ^ {m} \right] ^ {1 / m} \leq K m n _ {i - 1} ^ {- 1 / 2}.
$$

This implies  $\left|D_{j_i}^{(i)}\right|^{1/2}$  is  $Kn_{i-1}^{-1/4}$ -sub-Gaussian and hence for any  $\delta > 0$ ,

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\left| D _ {j _ {i}} ^ {(i)} \right| \geq \delta\right) \leq e \cdot \exp \left(- K \delta n _ {i - 1} ^ {1 / 2}\right).
$$

The union bound yields

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\left| \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} D _ {j _ {i}} ^ {(i)} \right| \geq \delta\right) \leq K n _ {i} \exp \left(- K \delta n _ {i - 1} ^ {1 / 2}\right).
$$

Note that this holds for any  $B > 0$ . The rest of the bounds are similarly proven.

Similar to Lemma 8, one can prove the following:

Lemma 52. Under Assumptions 1 and 3, for any  $t \in [0,\infty)$ ,

$$
\| \tilde {W} \| _ {t} \leq K ^ {\kappa_ {L}} \left(1 + t ^ {\kappa_ {L}}\right) \left(1 + \| \tilde {W} \| _ {0} ^ {\kappa_ {L}}\right), \quad \| \mathbf {W} \| _ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor} \leq K ^ {\kappa_ {L}} \left(1 + t ^ {\kappa_ {L}}\right) \left(1 + \| \mathbf {W} \| _ {0} ^ {\kappa_ {L}}\right),
$$

where  $\kappa_{L} = K^{L}$  for some constant  $K > 1$  sufficiently large. In particular, for any  $i\in [L]$ :

$$
\left(\frac{1}{n_{i}}\sum_{j_{i} = 1}^{n_{i}}\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i}^{\mathbf{H}}\left(Z,j_{i};\tilde{W}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{50}\right)^{1 / 50}\leq K^{\kappa_{L}}\left(1 + t^{\kappa_{L}}\right)\left(1 + \| |\tilde{W}\| \| _ {0}^{\kappa_{L}}\right),
$$

$$
\left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (\lfloor s / \epsilon \rfloor)) \right| ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0} \leq K ^ {\kappa_ {L}} (1 + t ^ {\kappa_ {L}}) (1 + \| | \mathbf {W} \| | _ {0} ^ {\kappa_ {L}}).
$$

Furthermore, by defining

$$
\| | W \| | _ {\mathrm {s a m p}, t} = \max \left(\max _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ \sup _ {s \leq t} | w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | ^ {5 0} \right]\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}, \right.
$$

$$
\max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} | w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i}) | ^ {5 0} \right]\right) ^ {1 / 5 0},
$$

$$
\max _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup _ {s \leq t} | w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0},
$$

$$
\max _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup _ {s \leq t} | b _ {i} (s, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}
$$

$$
\left(\frac {1}{n _ {1}} \sum_ {j _ {1} = 1} ^ {n _ {1}} \sup _ {s \leq t} | w _ {1} (s, C _ {1} (j _ {1})) | ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0},
$$

we also have:

$$
\| \| W \| \| _ {\operatorname {s a m p}, t} \leq K ^ {\kappa_ {L}} \left(1 + t ^ {\kappa_ {L}}\right) \left(1 + \max  \left(\| \| W \| \| _ {0} ^ {\kappa_ {L}}, \| \| W \| \| _ {\operatorname {s a m p}, 0} ^ {\kappa_ {L}}\right)\right),
$$

$$
\left(\frac{1}{n_{i}}\sum_{j_{i} = 1}^{n_{i}}\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right);W\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{50}\right)^{1 / 50}\leq K^{\kappa_{L}}\left(1 + t^{\kappa_{L}}\right)\left(1 + \max \left(\| \| W\| \|_{0}^{\kappa_{L}},\| \| W\| \|_{\mathrm{samp},0}^{\kappa_{L}}\right)\right).
$$

Proof. The proof follows the same argument as Lemma 8. This is obvious for the statements concerning  $\tilde{W}$  and  $\mathbf{W}$ . To prove the latter claims that involve  $\| |W\|_{\mathrm{samp},t}$ , the argument follows similarly. In particular, let us denote

$$
\| w _ {i} \| _ {\text {r i g h t}, t} = \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} | w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | ^ {5 0} \right] \Bigg) ^ {1 / 5 0}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
\| w _ {i} \| _ {\mathrm {l e f t}, t} = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} | w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i}) | ^ {5 0} \right]\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}, \qquad 2 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
\| | w _ {i} \| | _ {\mathrm {c e n}, t} = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup  _ {s \leq t} | w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
\| \| b _ {i} \| \| _ {\operatorname {s a m p}, t} = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup  _ {s \leq t} | b _ {i} (s, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
\| \left| w _ {1} \right| \| _ {\operatorname {s a m p}, t} = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {1}} \sum_ {j _ {1} = 1} ^ {n _ {1}} \sup  _ {s \leq t} | w _ {1} (s, C _ {1} (j _ {1})) | ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0},
$$

$$
\| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \| _ {\mathrm {s a m p}, t} = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i} (j _ {i}); W (s)) \right| ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}, \qquad 1 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
\| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \| _ {t} = \mathbb {E} \left[ \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup _ {s \leq t} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i}; W (s)) \right| ^ {5 0} \right] ^ {1 / 5 0}, \qquad 1 \leq i \leq L.
$$

Then similar to Lemma 8, we obtain for  $2 \leq i \leq L$ :

$$
\| \left\| \Delta_ {L} ^ {H} \right\| _ {\text {s a m p}, t} \leq K,
$$

$$
\| \Delta_ {i - 1} ^ {H} \| _ {\text {s a m p}, t} \leq K \left(1 + \| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \| _ {t}\right) \left(1 + \| w _ {i} \| _ {\text {l e f t}, t} + \| b _ {i} \| _ {t}\right),
$$

$$
\| \left. \left| \left| w _ {i} \right| \right| \right| _ {\text {l e f t}, t} \leq \| \left. \left| \left| w _ {i} \right| \right| \right| _ {\text {l e f t}, 0} + K \left(1 + \| \left. \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \right| \right| _ {t}\right) t,
$$

$$
\| \left\| w _ {i} \right\| _ {\text {r i g h t}, t} \leq \| \left\| w _ {i} \right\| _ {\text {r i g h t}, 0} + K \left(1 + \left\| \left. \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \right\| _ {\text {s a m p}, t}\right) t, \right.
$$

$$
\| \left. \left. w _ {i} \right\rVert \right\rVert_ {\text {c e n}, t} \leq \| \left. w _ {i} \right\rVert \| _ {\text {c e n}, 0} + K \left(1 + \| \left. \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \right\rVert \| _ {\text {s a m p}, t}\right) t,
$$

$$
\left\| \left| b _ {i} \right| \right\| _ {\text {s a m p}, t} \leq \left\| \left| b _ {i} \right| \right\| _ {\text {s a m p}, 0} + K \left(1 + \left\| \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \right| \right\| _ {\text {s a m p}, t}\right) t,
$$

$$
\| \left. w _ {1} \right\| _ {\text {s a m p}, t} \leq \| \left. w _ {1} \right\| _ {\text {s a m p}, 0} + K \left(1 + \| \left. \Delta_ {1} ^ {H} \right\| _ {\text {s a m p}, t}\right) t.
$$

Note that

$$
\| \| W \| \| _ {\text {s a m p}, t} = \max  \left(\max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \| \| w _ {i} \| \| _ {\text {l e f t}, t}, \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \| \| w _ {i} \| \| _ {\text {r i g h t}, t}, \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \| \| w _ {i} \| \| _ {\text {c e n}, t}, \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \| \| b _ {i} \| \| _ {\text {s a m p}, t}, \| \| w _ {1} \| \| _ {\text {s a m p}, t}\right).
$$

Together with the bound on  $\| \Delta_i^H\| _t$  given by Lemma 8, one can derive the claims.
