# C.1.1 Proof of Proposition 22

Proof of Proposition 22. In the following, let  $K_{t}$  denote an immaterial positive constant that takes the form

$$
K _ {t} = K ^ {\kappa_ {L}} \left(1 + t ^ {\kappa_ {L}}\right),
$$

where  $\kappa_{L} = K^{L}$ , such that  $K_{t} \geq 1$  and  $K_{t} \leq K_{T}$  for all  $t \leq T$ . We note that the terminal time  $T$ , the constant  $K_{t}$ , as well as the usual immaterial constant  $K$ , do not depend on  $B$ . We start with some preliminary facts:

Fact 1: moment bounds. We first note that at initialization,  $\mathcal{D}_0(W,\tilde{W}) = 0$  and  $\| W\| _0\leq K$ . By Assumption 5 and Lemma 8,  $\| W\| _T\leq K_T$ . Furthermore, by Lemma 51, with probability at least  $1 - KLn_{\mathrm{max}}\exp \left(-Kn_{\mathrm{min}}^{1 / 52}\right)$ , we have  $\| \tilde{W}\| _0\leq K$  and  $\| W\|_{\mathrm{samp},0}\leq K$ , recalling the definition of  $\| W\|_{\mathrm{samp},t}$  from the statement of Lemma 52. Let this event be denoted by  $\mathcal{E}$ . Unless noticed otherwise, we shall place most of the contexts of our proof upon  $\mathcal{E}$ . By Lemma 52, one deduces that

$$
\| \tilde {W} \| _ {T}, \| W \| _ {\operatorname {s a m p}, T} \leq K _ {T},
$$

$$
\left(\frac{1}{n_{i}}\sum_{j_{i} = 1}^{n_{i}}\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i}^{\mathbf{H}}\left(Z,j_{i};\tilde{W}\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{50}\right)^{1 / 50}\leq K_{T},
$$

$$
\left(\frac{1}{n_{i}}\sum_{j_{i} = 1}^{n_{i}}\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right);W\left(s\right)\right)\right|^{50}\right)^{1 / 50}\leq K_{T}
$$

on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ . We also remark that the fact  $\| W \|_T \leq K_T$  holds irrespective of  $\mathcal{E}$ .

Fact 2: maximal bounds for  $W$ . We note that the assumption  $\operatorname{ess - supmax}_0^w(W)$  and  $\operatorname{ess - supmax}_0^b(W)$  has an interesting consequence:

$$
\operatorname {e s s - s u p m a x} _ {T} ^ {w} (W) \leq K _ {T} (1 + B),
$$

$$
\operatorname {e s s - s u p m a x} _ {T} ^ {b} (W) \leq K _ {T} (1 + B),
$$

$$
\operatorname *{ess -sup}\limits_{1\leq i\leq L}\max \limits_{t\leq T}\left|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i};W\left(t\right)\right)\right|\leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right).
$$

We note that this claim holds irrespective of the event  $\mathcal{E}$  from Fact 1. Following this claim, it is immediate that almost surely,

$$
\operatorname *{ess -sup}_{C_{i}}\max_{2\leq i\leq L}\max_{j_{i - 1}\in [n_{i - 1}]}|w_{i}\left(t,C_{i - 1}\left(j_{i - 1}\right),C_{i}\right)|\leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right),
$$

$$
\operatorname *{ess -sup}_{C_{i - 1}}\max_{2\leq i\leq L}\max_{j_{i}\in [n_{i}]}|w_{i}\left(t,C_{i - 1},C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right)\right)|\leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right),
$$

$$
\max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \max  _ {j _ {i - 1} \in [ n _ {i - 1} ], j _ {i} \in [ n _ {i} ]} | w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | \leq K _ {T} (1 + B),
$$

$$
\max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \max  _ {j _ {i} \in [ n _ {i} ]} | b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | \leq K _ {T} (1 + B),
$$

$$
\operatorname *{ess -sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\max_{1\leq i\leq L}\max_{j_{i}\in [n_{i}]} \sup_{t\leq T}\bigl|\Delta_{i}^{H}(Z,C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right);W\left( \right.t)\bigr)\bigr|\leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right),
$$

since  $C_i(j_i)$  is a copy of  $C_i$ . Now we prove the claim. First consider  $\max_t^w(W)$ . By Assumption 3, for  $\mathcal{P}$ -almost every  $z$ ,

$$
\sup_{t\geq 0}\sup_{c_{L - 1}\in \Omega_{L - 1}}|\Delta^{w}_{L}(z,c_{L - 1},1;W (t))|\\ \leq K\left(1 + \sup_{t\geq 0}\bigl|\Delta^{H}_{L}(z,1;W (t))\bigr |\right)\leq K,
$$

which implies, by Assumption 1, that

$$
\operatorname *{ess -sup}\limits_{t\leq T}|w_{L}\left(t,C_{L - 1},1\right)|\leq \operatorname *{ess -sup}\left|w_{L}^{0}\left(C_{L - 1},1\right)\right| + KT\leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right).
$$

Next assuming that  $\operatorname{ess-sup} \sup_{t \leq T} |w_i(t, C_{i-1}, C_i)| \leq K_T(1 + B)$  for a given  $i \geq 2$ , by Assumption 3, we have for  $\mathcal{P}$ -almost every  $z$  and all  $t \leq T$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \Delta_ {i - 1} ^ {H} (z, C _ {i - 1}; W (t)) \right| \leq K \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ \right.\left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (z, C _ {i}; W (t)) \right|\right) (1 + | w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) | + | b _ {i} (t, C _ {i}) |\left. \right)\left. \right] \\ \leq K \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (z, C _ {i}; W (t)) \right|\right) \left(K _ {T} (1 + B) + | b _ {i} (t, C _ {i}) |\right) \right] \\ \leq K \left[ \left(1 + \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (z, C _ {i}; W (t)) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}\right) \left(K _ {T} (1 + B) + \mathbb {E} \left[ \left| b _ {i} (t, C _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}\right) \right] \\ \leq K _ {T} (1 + B), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last step follows from the fact  $\| |W\| |_T\leq K_T$  and Lemma 8. Again by Assumption 3, we then obtain:

$$
\left| \Delta_ {i - 1} ^ {w} (z, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}; W (t)) \right| \leq K _ {T} (1 + B),
$$

which implies, by Assumption 1, that

$$
\operatorname * {e s s - \sup } _ {t \leq T} \sup  _ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left| w _ {i - 1} \left(t, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right| \leq \operatorname * {e s s - \sup } _ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left| w _ {i - 1} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right| + K _ {T} (1 + B) T \leq K _ {T} (1 + B).
$$

This completes the induction argument to show that  $\operatorname{ess-supmax}_t^w(W) \leq K_T(1 + B)$ . We have also showed that

$$
\operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {1 \leq i \leq L} \max  _ {t \leq T} \sup  _ {i} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i}; W (t)) \right| \leq K _ {T} (1 + B).
$$

We thus obtain from Assumption 3:

$$
\left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {b} (z, C _ {i}; W (t)) \right| \leq K _ {T} (1 + B),
$$

for  $2 \leq i \leq L$  and  $\mathcal{P}$ -almost every  $z$ . This implies:

$$
\operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {t \leq T} \left| b _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i}\right) \right| \leq \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} \left| b _ {i} ^ {0} \left(C _ {i}\right) \right| + K _ {T} (1 + B) T \leq K _ {T} (1 + B),
$$

which shows  $\operatorname{ess -supmax}_t^b (W) \leq K_T(1 + B)$ , as claimed.

Fact 3: maximal bounds for  $\tilde{W}$ . We also have on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ , almost surely,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \max  _ {j _ {i - 1} \in [ n _ {i - 1} ], j _ {i} \in [ n _ {i} ]} \sup  _ {t \leq T} | \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | \leq K _ {T} (1 + B), \\ \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \max  _ {j _ {i} \in [ n _ {i} ]} \sup  _ {t \leq T} \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) \right| \leq K _ {T} (1 + B). \\ \end{array}
$$

A proof of this fact is similar to the argument for Fact 2. We note that this argument requires the use of the fact  $\| \tilde{W} \|_T \leq K_T$ , which holds on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ , and the application of Lemma 8. The latter application holds by noticing that  $\tilde{W}$  can be viewed as a collection of MF parameter whose neuronal ensemble  $(\Omega_{\mathrm{new}}, P_{\mathrm{new}}) = \prod_{i=1}^{L} (\Omega_{i,\mathrm{new}}, P_{i,\mathrm{new}})$  takes the following specific form:  $\Omega_{i,\mathrm{new}} = \{C_i(1), \dots, C_i(n_i)\}$  and  $P_{i,\mathrm{new}}$  is a uniform probability measure on  $\Omega_{i,\mathrm{new}}$ .

We now decompose the proof into several steps.

Step 1 - Main proof. Let us first define some quantities that represent the difference between  $W$  and  $\tilde{W}$ :<sup>4</sup>

$$
D _ {i} ^ {w} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} | \partial_ {1} \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) - \partial_ {1} w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
D _ {i} ^ {b} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| \partial_ {1} \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) - \partial_ {1} b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
D _ {1} ^ {w} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {1}} \sum_ {j _ {1} = 1} ^ {n _ {1}} | \partial_ {1} \tilde {w} _ {1} (t, j _ {1}) - \partial_ {1} w _ {1} (t, C _ {1} (j _ {1})) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}.
$$

We are also interested in the following quantities that represent the smoothness in the time evolution of  $W(t)$  and  $\tilde{W}(t)$ :

$$
A _ {i} ^ {w} (t, \zeta) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| \partial_ {1} w _ {i} (t + \zeta , C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) - \partial_ {1} w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
\tilde {A} _ {i} ^ {w} (t, \zeta) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} | \partial_ {1} \tilde {w} _ {i} (t + \zeta , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) - \partial_ {1} \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
A _ {i} ^ {b} (t, \zeta) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| \partial_ {1} b _ {i} (t + \zeta , C _ {i} (j _ {i})) - \partial_ {1} b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
\tilde {A} _ {i} ^ {b} (t, \zeta) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| \partial_ {1} \tilde {b} _ {i} (t + \zeta , j _ {i}) - \partial_ {1} \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}, \quad 2 \leq i \leq L,
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} A _ {1} ^ {w} (t, \zeta) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {1}} \sum_ {j _ {1} = 1} ^ {n _ {1}} | \partial_ {1} w _ {1} (t + \zeta , C _ {1} (j _ {1})) - \partial_ {1} w _ {1} (t, C _ {1} (j _ {1})) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \tilde {A} _ {1} ^ {w} (t, \zeta) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {1}} \sum_ {j _ {1} = 1} ^ {n _ {1}} \left| \partial_ {1} \tilde {w} _ {1} (t + \zeta , j _ {1}) - \partial_ {1} \tilde {w} _ {1} (t, j _ {1}) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

These quantities give a bound on  $\mathcal{D}_t(W, \tilde{W})$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right) \leq K \int_ {0} ^ {t} \max _ {1 \leq i \leq L} D _ {i} ^ {w} \left(\lfloor s / \zeta \rfloor \zeta\right) d s + K \int_ {0} ^ {t} \max _ {2 \leq i \leq L} D _ {i} ^ {b} \left(\lfloor s / \zeta \rfloor \zeta\right) d s \\ + K t \sup  _ {s \leq T - \zeta} \sup  _ {0 \leq \zeta^ {\prime} \leq \zeta} \max  _ {i} \left(A _ {i} ^ {w} (s, \zeta^ {\prime}), A _ {i} ^ {b} (s, \zeta^ {\prime}), \tilde {A} _ {i} ^ {w} (s, \zeta^ {\prime}), \tilde {A} _ {i} ^ {b} (s, \zeta^ {\prime})\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where we have used the fact  $\mathcal{D}_0(W, \tilde{W}) = 0$ . The next task is to bound the terms inside the integral.

To find bounds on  $D_{i}^{w}(t)$ , we introduce the quantities for  $1 \leq i \leq L$ :

$$
G _ {i} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\textbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (t)) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i} (j _ {i}); W (t)) \right| \right] ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2},
$$

$$
F _ {i} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i} (X, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (t)) - H _ {i} (X, C _ {i} (j _ {i}); W (t)) \right| \right] ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}.
$$

We specify their connection in the following. By Assumptions 1 and 3, for  $i \geq 2$

$$
D _ {i} ^ {w} (t) \leq K \left(D _ {i} ^ {w, 1} (t) + G _ {i} (t) + \mathcal {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right) + F _ {i} (t)\right),
$$

in which

$$
\begin{array}{l} D _ {i} ^ {w, 1} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \underset {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} {\mathrm {e s s - s u p}} \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (t)) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, C _ {i} (j _ {i}); W (t)) \right| ^ {2}\right) \right. \\ \times \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[\left| \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} (X, j _ {i - 1}; \tilde {W} (t)) - H _ {i - 1} (X, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}); W (t)) \right|\right] ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

By Lemma 52, on the event  $\mathcal{E}$

$$
D _ {i} ^ {w, 1} (t) \leq K _ {T} F _ {i - 1} (t).
$$

As such, on the event  $\mathcal{E}$

$$
D _ {i} ^ {w} (t) \leq K _ {T} \left(F _ {i - 1} (t) + G _ {i} (t) + \mathcal {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right) + F _ {i} (t)\right).
$$

Similarly, we also have:

$$
D _ {1} ^ {w} (t) \leq K \left(G _ {1} (t) + \mathcal {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right)\right).
$$

Together with the previously derived bound on  $\mathcal{D}_t(W, \tilde{W})$ , we obtain on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathscr {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right) \leq K _ {T} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathscr {D} _ {s} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right) d s + K _ {T} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \max  _ {i} \left(G _ {i} \left(\lfloor s / \zeta \rfloor \zeta\right) + F _ {i} \left(\lfloor s / \zeta \rfloor \zeta\right)\right) d s \\ + K \int_ {0} ^ {t} \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} D _ {i} ^ {b} (\lfloor s / \zeta \rfloor \zeta) d s \\ + K t \sup  _ {s \leq T - \zeta} \sup  _ {0 \leq \zeta^ {\prime} \leq \zeta} \max  _ {i} \left(A _ {i} ^ {w} (s, \zeta^ {\prime}), A _ {i} ^ {b} (s, \zeta^ {\prime}), \tilde {A} _ {i} ^ {w} (s, \zeta^ {\prime}), \tilde {A} _ {i} ^ {b} (s, \zeta^ {\prime})\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

which holds for all  $t \leq T$ .

Next we make the following claims:

- Claim 1: For any  $\zeta \in [0,T]$ , on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ , almost surely,

$$
\sup_{t\leq T - \zeta}\sup_{0\leq \zeta^{\prime}\leq \zeta}\max_{i}\left(A_{i}^{w}\left(t,\zeta^{\prime}\right),A_{i}^{b}\left(t,\zeta^{\prime}\right),\tilde{A}_{i}^{w}\left(t,\zeta^{\prime}\right),\tilde{A}_{i}^{b}\left(t,\zeta^{\prime}\right)\right)\\ \leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right)\zeta .
$$

- Claim 2: For a sequence  $\{\gamma_j > 0, j = 2,\dots,L\}$  and  $t \leq T$ , let  $\mathcal{E}_{t,i}^{\mathrm{H}}$  denote the event in which for all  $k \in \{1,2,\dots,i\}$ ,

$$
F _ {k} (t) \leq K _ {T} ^ {k} \bigg (\mathcal {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + (1 + B) \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {k - 1} \gamma_ {j + 1} \bigg).
$$

(The summation  $\sum_{j=1}^{k-1}$  equals 0 if  $k = 1$ .) We claim that for each  $i = 1, \dots, L$ :

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {t, i} ^ {\mathrm {H}}; \mathcal {E}\right) \geq 1 - \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {i - 1} \frac {n _ {j + 1}}{\gamma_ {j + 1}} \exp \left(- n _ {j} \gamma_ {j + 1} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right).
$$

- Claim 3: For a sequence  $\{\beta_j > 0, j = 1,\dots,L - 2\}$  and  $t \leq T$ , let  $\mathcal{E}_{t,i}^{\Delta}$  denote the event that for all  $k \in \{i,i + 1,\dots,L\}$ ,

$$
G _ {k} (t) \leq K _ {T} ^ {2 L - k + 1} \Bigg ((1 + B) \mathscr {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + (1 + B ^ {2}) (\delta_ {L} ^ {\Delta} + \sum_ {j = k} ^ {L - 2} \beta_ {j}) \Bigg),
$$

where  $\delta_L^\Delta = \sum_{j=1}^{L-1} \gamma_{j+1}$ . (The summation  $\sum_{j=k}^{L-2}$  equals 0 if  $k \geq L-1$ .) We claim that for each  $i = 1, \ldots, L$ :

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {t, L} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \cap \mathcal {E} _ {t, i} ^ {\Delta}; \mathcal {E}\right) \geq \mathbb {P} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {t, L} ^ {\mathbf {H}}; \mathcal {E}\right) - \sum_ {j = i} ^ {L - 2} \frac {n _ {j}}{\beta_ {j}} \exp \left(- n _ {j + 1} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right).
$$

- Claim 4: For  $t \leq T$ , let  $\mathcal{E}_t^b$  denote the event that for all  $k \in \{2, \dots, L\}$ ,

$$
D _ {k} ^ {b} (t) \leq K _ {T} \left((1 + B) \mathscr {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + (1 + B ^ {2}) \delta_ {L} ^ {b}\right),
$$

where  $\delta_L^b = \delta_L^\Delta +\sum_{j = 1}^{L - 2}\beta_j$  . We claim that

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {t, L} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \cap \mathcal {E} _ {t, 1} ^ {\Delta} \cap \mathcal {E} _ {t} ^ {b}; \mathcal {E}\right) \geq \mathbb {P} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {t, L} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \cap \mathcal {E} _ {t, 1} ^ {\Delta}; \mathcal {E}\right) - \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {i - 1} \frac {n _ {j + 1}}{\gamma_ {j + 1}} \exp \left(- n _ {j} \gamma_ {j + 1} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right).
$$

Let us assume these claims. Using the bounds on  $\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathbf{H}};\mathcal{E}\right)$ ,  $\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathbf{H}}\cap \mathcal{E}_{t,1}^{\Delta};\mathcal{E}\right)$  and  $\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathbf{H}}\cap \mathcal{E}_{t,1}^{\Delta}\cap \mathcal{E}_t^b;\mathcal{E}\right)$  combining the previous bound, applying the union bound over  $t\in \{0,\zeta ,2\zeta ,\dots,\lfloor T / \zeta \rfloor \zeta \}$  and recalling  $\mathcal{D}_0(W,\tilde{W}) = 0$ , we then get:

$$
\mathcal {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right) \leq K _ {T} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ (1 + B) \mathcal {D} _ {s} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right) + \left(1 + B ^ {2}\right) \left(\sum_ {j = 1} ^ {L - 1} \gamma_ {j + 1} + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {L - 2} \beta_ {j}\right) + (1 + B) \zeta \right] d s,
$$

for all  $t \leq T$ , with probability at least

$$
1 - \frac {T}{\zeta} \left(\sum_ {j = 1} ^ {L - 1} \frac {n _ {j + 1}}{\gamma_ {j + 1}} \exp \left(- n _ {j} \gamma_ {j + 1} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right) + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {L - 2} \frac {n _ {j}}{\beta_ {j}} \exp \left(- n _ {j + 1} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right)\right) - K L n _ {\max} \exp \left(- K n _ {\min} ^ {1 / 5 2}\right),
$$

for any  $\zeta \in [0,T]$ . By Gronwall's lemma, the above implies that for all  $t\leq T$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathscr {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) \leq K _ {T} \left[ (1 + B ^ {2}) \left(\sum_ {j = 1} ^ {L - 1} \gamma_ {j + 1} + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {L - 2} \beta_ {j}\right) + (1 + B) \zeta \right] \exp \left(K _ {T} (1 + B) T\right) \\ \leq K _ {T} \left(\sum_ {j = 1} ^ {L - 1} \gamma_ {j + 1} + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {L - 2} \beta_ {j} + \zeta\right) \exp \left(K _ {T} (1 + B)\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

The proposition statement is then easily obtained by choosing

$$
\begin{array}{l} \gamma_ {j + 1} = \sqrt {\frac {1}{K _ {T} n _ {j}} \log \left(\frac {2 T L n _ {\mathrm {m a x}} ^ {2}}{\delta} + e\right)}, \quad j = 1, \dots , L - 1, \\ \beta_ {j} = \sqrt {\frac {1}{K _ {T} n _ {j + 1}} \log \left(\frac {2 T L n _ {\mathrm {m a x}} ^ {2}}{\delta} + e\right)}, \quad j = 1, \dots , L - 2, \\ \zeta = 1 / \sqrt {n _ {\max}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

We are left with verifying the claims.

Step 2 - Claim 1. We first note that by Assumptions 1 and 3, Lemma 52, and the fact  $\| W\|_0$ ,  $\| W\|_{\mathrm{samp},0} \leq K$  on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup  _ {s \leq t} \left| \partial_ {1} w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0} \\ \leq K + K \Bigg(\frac{1}{n_{i}}\sum_{j_{i} = 1}^{n_{i}}\sup_{s\leq t}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\big|\Delta_{i}^{H}\left(Z,C_{i}\left(j_{i}\right);W\left(s\right)\right)\big|^{50}\Bigg)^{1 / 50}\leq K_{T}, \\ \end{array}
$$

for any  $t \leq T$ . Therefore,

$$
\left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup  _ {s \leq T - \zeta} \sup  _ {0 \leq \zeta^ {\prime} \leq \zeta} \left| w _ {i} \left(s + \zeta^ {\prime}, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) - w _ {i} \left(s, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} \zeta .
$$

We also have similarly that on the event  $\mathcal{E}$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq T - \zeta} \sup  _ {0 \leq \zeta^ {\prime} \leq \zeta} \left| w _ {i} (s + \zeta^ {\prime}, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) - w _ {i} (s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2} \right]\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} \zeta , \\ \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq T - \zeta} \sup  _ {0 \leq \zeta^ {\prime} \leq \zeta} \left| w _ {i} \left(s + \zeta^ {\prime}, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i}\right) - w _ {i} \left(s, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {2} \right]\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} \zeta , \\ \mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq T - \zeta} \sup  _ {0 \leq \zeta^ {\prime} \leq \zeta} \left| w _ {i} \left(s + \zeta^ {\prime}, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) - w _ {i} \left(s, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} \zeta , \\ \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup  _ {s \leq T - \zeta} \sup  _ {0 \leq \zeta^ {\prime} \leq \zeta} \left| b _ {i} (s + \zeta^ {\prime}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) - b _ {i} (s, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} \zeta , \\ \mathbb{E}\left[ \sup_{s\leq T - \zeta}\sup_{0\leq \zeta^{\prime}\leq \zeta}\left|b_{i}\left(s + \zeta^{\prime},C_{i}\right) - b_{i}\left(s,C_{i}\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1 / 2}\leq K_{T}\zeta , \\ \left(\frac {1}{n _ {1}} \sum_ {j _ {1} = 1} ^ {n _ {1}} \sup  _ {s \leq t} \sup  _ {0 \leq \zeta^ {\prime} \leq \zeta} \left| w _ {1} \left(s + \zeta^ {\prime}, C _ {1} (j _ {1})\right) - w _ {1} (s, C _ {1} (j _ {1})) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} \zeta , \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ \sup  _ {s \leq t} \sup  _ {0 \leq \zeta^ {\prime} \leq \zeta} \left| w _ {1} (s + \zeta^ {\prime}, C _ {1}) - w _ {1} (s, C _ {1}) \right| ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} \zeta .
$$

Together with Lemma 49, this fact gives us a bound on  $A_i^w(t,\zeta)$ . In particular, defining  $W_{\zeta}(t) = W(t + \zeta)$ , we apply Lemma 49 to the two MF parameter collections  $W$  and  $W_{\zeta}$  along with the new random variable  $\tilde{C}_i$  that is drawn uniformly from the set  $\{C_i(1),\dots,C_i(n_i)\}$ . Recalling the metric  $\tilde{d}_t(W,W_{\zeta})$  in this lemma, the above fact shows that  $\tilde{d}_{T - \zeta}(W,W_{\zeta}) \leq K_T\zeta$  on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ . The lemma holds owing to Fact 1 and Fact 2. The conclusion of the lemma then reads as

$$
\sup_{t\leq T - \zeta}\sup_{0\leq \zeta^{\prime}\leq \zeta}\max_{i}\left(A_{i}^{w}\left(t,\zeta^{\prime}\right),A_{i}^{b}\left(t,\zeta^{\prime}\right)\right)\\ \leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right)\tilde{d}_{T - \zeta}\left(W,W_{\zeta}\right)\leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right)\zeta ,
$$

almost surely on the event  $\mathcal{E}$

By a similar argument, we have almost surely on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\sup_{t\leq T - \zeta}\sup_{0\leq \zeta^{\prime}\leq \zeta}\max_{i}\left(\tilde{A}_{i}^{w}\left(t,\zeta^{\prime}\right),\tilde{A}_{i}^{b}\left(t,\zeta^{\prime}\right)\right) \leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right)\zeta .
$$

Indeed one can repeat the argument here by noticing that  $\tilde{W}$  can be viewed as a collection of MF parameter whose neuronal ensemble  $(\Omega_{\mathrm{new}},P_{\mathrm{new}}) = \prod_{i = 1}^{L}(\Omega_{i,\mathrm{new}},P_{i,\mathrm{new}})$  takes the following specific form:  $\Omega_{i,\mathrm{new}} = \{C_i(1),\dots,C_i(n_i)\}$  and  $P_{i,\mathrm{new}}$  is a uniform probability measure on  $\Omega_{i,\mathrm{new}}$ .

Step 3 - Claim 2. We show the claim by induction. Consider  $F_{1}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \mathbf {H} _ {1} (x, j _ {1}; \tilde {W} (t)) - H _ {1} (x, C _ {i} (j _ {i}); W (t)) \right| = | \phi_ {1} (\tilde {w} _ {1} (t, j _ {1}), x) - \phi_ {1} (w _ {1} (t, C _ {1} (j _ {1})), x) | \\ \leq K \mathcal {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) \\ \end{array}
$$

for  $\mathcal{P}$ -almost every  $x$  by Assumption 2, and therefore,

$$
F _ {1} (t) \leq K \mathcal {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}).
$$

That is,  $\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{t,1}^{\mathbf{H}}\right) = 1$

Now let us assume the claim for  $F_{i-1}$  with  $i \geq 2$  and consider the claim for  $F_i$ . We have the following decomposition:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i} \left(X, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (t)\right) - H _ {i} \left(X, C _ {i} (j _ {i}); W (t)\right) \right| \\ = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \phi_ {i} \left(\tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}), \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}), \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} (X, j _ {i - 1}; \tilde {W} (t))\right) \right. \\ \left. - \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ \phi_ {i} \left(w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , H _ {i - 1} (X, C _ {i - 1}; W (t))\right) \right] \right| \\ \leq Q _ {1, i} (t) + Q _ {2, i} (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

which gives

$$
F _ {i} (t) \leq \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ | Q _ {1, i} (s) | + | Q _ {2, i} (s) | \right] ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2},
$$

where we define

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {1, i} (t) = \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left| \phi_ {i} \left(\tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}), \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}), \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} (X, j _ {i - 1}; \tilde {W} (t))\right) \right. \\ \left. - \phi_ {i} \left(w _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), b _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), H _ {i - 1} \left(X, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right); W (t)\right)\right) \right|, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {2, i} (t) = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \phi_ {i} \left(w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , H _ {i - 1} (X, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}); W (t))\right) \right. \\ \left. - \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ \phi_ {i} \left(w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , H _ {i - 1} (X, C _ {i - 1}; W (t))\right) \right] \right|. \\ \end{array}
$$

By Assumption 2 and Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality, we obtain a bound on  $Q_{1,i}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ | Q _ {1, i} (t) | \right] ^ {2} \leq \frac {K}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left(1 + \left| \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2}\right) \\ \times \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} (X, j _ {i - 1}; \tilde {W} (t)) - H _ {i - 1} (X, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}); W (t)) \right| \right] ^ {2} \\ + \frac {K}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} | \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) - w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | ^ {2} \\ + K \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) - b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

and therefore, by Fact 1, under the events  $\mathcal{E}_{t,i - 1}^{\mathrm{H}}$  and  $\mathcal{E}$

$$
\left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ | Q _ {1, i} (t) | \right] ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} F _ {i - 1} (t) + K \mathcal {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right).
$$

Let us bound  $Q_{2,i}$ . For brevity, let us write

$$
Z _ {i} ^ {H} \left(t, c _ {i - 1}, c _ {i}\right) = \phi_ {i} \left(w _ {i} \left(t, c _ {i - 1}, c _ {i}\right), b _ {i} \left(t, c _ {i}\right), H _ {i - 1} \left(x, c _ {i - 1}; W (t)\right)\right).
$$

Recall that  $C_{i-1}(j_{i-1})$  and  $C_i(j_i)$  are independent. We thus have:

$$
\mathbb {E} \left[ Z _ {i} ^ {H} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | C _ {i} (j _ {i}) \right] = \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ Z _ {i} ^ {H} (t, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right].
$$

Furthermore  $\{C_{i - 1}(j_{i - 1})\}_{j_{i - 1}\in [n_{i - 1}]}$  are  $\eta_{i - 1}$ -independent by Assumption 4. We also have that for  $\mathcal{P}$ -almost every  $x$ , almost surely,

$$
\left| Z _ {i} ^ {H} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right| \leq K _ {T} (1 + B),
$$

by Assumption 2 and Fact 2. Then by Lemma 50, noting that  $\gamma_{i}\geq K\eta_{i - 1}$

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ Q _ {2, i} \right] \geq K _ {T} (1 + B) \gamma_ {i}) \leq (1 / \gamma_ {i}) \exp \left(- n _ {i - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right). \right.
$$

By taking a union bound of the above probabilistic bound over  $j_{i}\in [n_{i}]$ , we thus have, on the events  $\mathcal{E}_{t,i - 1}^{\mathrm{H}}$  and  $\mathcal{E}$

$$
\begin{array}{l} F _ {i} (t) \leq K _ {T} F _ {i - 1} (t) + K \mathscr {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + K _ {T} (1 + B) \gamma_ {i} \\ \leq K _ {T} ^ {i} \left(\mathcal {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + (1 + B) \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {i - 1} \gamma_ {j + 1}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

with probability at least  $1 - (n_i / \gamma_i)\exp \left(-n_{i - 1}\gamma_i^2 /K_T\right)$ . We thus get:

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {t, i} ^ {\mathbf {H}}; \mathcal {E}\right) \geq \mathbb {P} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {t, i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {H}}; \mathcal {E}\right) - \frac {n _ {j}}{\gamma_ {j}} \exp \left(- n _ {i - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right) \geq 1 - \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {i - 1} \frac {n _ {j + 1}}{\gamma_ {j + 1}} \exp \left(- n _ {j} \gamma_ {j + 1} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right),
$$

which proves the claim.

Step 4 - Claim 3. We show the claim by backward induction. The proof is similar to Claim 2. Consider  $i = L$ . Notice that on the event  $\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathrm{H}}$ ,

$$
\mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left| \hat {\mathbf {y}} (X; \tilde {W} (t)) - \hat {y} (X; W (t)) \right| \right] \leq K F _ {L} (t) \leq K _ {T} ^ {L} \left(\mathcal {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + (1 + B) \delta_ {L} ^ {\Delta}\right),
$$

by Assumption 2. We thus get from Assumption 3 that on the events  $\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathbf{H}}$  and  $\mathcal{E}$

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {L} (t) \leq K \left(F _ {L} (t) + \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left| \hat {\mathbf {y}} (X; \tilde {W} (t)) - \hat {y} (X; W (t)) \right| \right]\right) \\ \leq K _ {T} ^ {L + 1} \Big (\mathscr {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + (1 + B) \delta_ {L} ^ {\Delta} \Big). \\ \end{array}
$$

That is,  $\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathbf{H}}\cap \mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\Delta};\mathcal{E}\right) = \mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathbf{H}};\mathcal{E}\right)$ .

Considering  $i = L - 1$ , by Assumption 3, we have:

$$
G _ {L - 1} (t) \leq K \left(G _ {L - 1} ^ {(1)} (t) + G _ {L - 1} ^ {(2)} (t) + G _ {L - 1} ^ {(3)} (t) + G _ {L - 1} ^ {(4)} (t)\right),
$$

in which

$$
G _ {L - 1} ^ {(1)} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {L - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {L - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {L - 1}} \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {L} (t, j _ {L - 1}, 1) | ^ {2} + | w _ {L} (t, C _ {L - 1} (j _ {L - 1}), 1) | ^ {2} + \left| \tilde {b} _ {L} (t, 1) \right| ^ {2} + | b _ {L} (t, 1) | ^ {2}\right)\right) ^ {1 / 2} G _ {L} (t),
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {L - 1} ^ {(2)} (t) = \left(1 + \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, 1; \tilde {W} (t)) \right| \right] + \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {H} (Z, 1; W (t)) \right| \right]\right) \\ \times \left(\frac {1}{n _ {L - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {L - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {L - 1}} | \tilde {w} _ {L} (t, j _ {L - 1}, 1) - w _ {L} (t, C _ {L - 1} (j _ {L - 1}), 1) | ^ {2} + \left| \tilde {b} _ {L} (t, 1) - b _ {L} (t, 1) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {L - 1} ^ {(3)} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {L - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {L - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {L - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \Big [ \Big (1 + \Big | \Delta_ {L} ^ {\textbf {H}} (Z, 1; \tilde {W} (t)) \Big | + \big | \Delta_ {L} ^ {H} (Z, 1; W (t)) \big |\right) \\ \times \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {L} (t, j _ {L - 1}, 1) | + | w _ {L} (t, C _ {L - 1} (j _ {L - 1}), 1) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {L} (t, 1) \right| + | b _ {L} (t, 1) |\right) \\ \times \left| \mathbf {H} _ {L} \left(X, 1; \tilde {W} (t)\right) - H _ {L} \left(X, 1; W (t)\right)\right| \bigg ] ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {L - 1} ^ {(4)} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {L - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {L - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {L - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, 1; \tilde {W} (t)) \right|\right) + \left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {H} (Z, 1; W (t)) \right|\right) \right. \\ \times \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {L} (t, j _ {L - 1}, 1) | + | w _ {L} (t, C _ {L - 1} (j _ {L - 1}), 1) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {L} (t, 1) \right| + | b _ {L} (t, 1) |\right) \\ \left. \times \left| \mathbf {H} _ {L - 1} \left(X, j _ {L - 1}; \tilde {W} (t)\right) - H _ {L} \left(X, C _ {L - 1} (j _ {L - 1}); W (t)\right)\right|\right] ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

Due to Fact 1, on the event  $\mathcal{E}$

$$
G _ {L - 1} ^ {(1)} (t) \leq K _ {T} G _ {L} (t).
$$

By Assumption 3, we have for  $\mathcal{P}$ -almost every  $z$ ,

$$
\left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (z, 1; \tilde {W} (t)) \right|, \left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {H} (z, 1; W (t)) \right| \leq K.
$$

Using this fact,

$$
G _ {L - 1} ^ {(2)} (t) \leq K \mathcal {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right).
$$

The same fact also applies to  $G_{L-1}^{(3)}$ ,  $G_{L-1}^{(4)}$  and  $G_{L-1}^{(5)}$ . In particular, we obtain for  $G_{L-1}^{(3)}$ , on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {L - 1} ^ {(3)} (t) \leq K \left(\frac {1}{n _ {L - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {L - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {L - 1}} \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {L} (t, j _ {L - 1}, 1) | + | w _ {L} (t, C _ {L - 1} (j _ {L - 1}), 1) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {L} (t, 1) \right| + | b _ {L} (t, 1) |\right) ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} F _ {L} (t) \\ \leq K _ {T} F _ {L} (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last display follows from Fact 1. Similarly, by using Fact 2 and Fact 3, we have on the event  $\mathcal{E}$

$$
G _ {L - 1} ^ {(4)} (t) \leq K _ {T} (1 + B) F _ {L - 1} (t).
$$

Hence on the events  $\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathbf{H}}$ ,  $\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\Delta}$  and  $\mathcal{E}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {L - 1} (t) \leq K _ {T} \left(G _ {L} (t) + \mathscr {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + F _ {L} (t) + (1 + B) F _ {L - 1} (t)\right) \\ \leq K _ {T} ^ {L + 2} \Bigg ((1 + B) \mathscr {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + (1 + B ^ {2}) \delta_ {L} ^ {\Delta} \Bigg). \\ \end{array}
$$

In other words,  $\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathbf{H}}\cap \mathcal{E}_{t,L - 1}^{\Delta};\mathcal{E}\right) = \mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathbf{H}};\mathcal{E}\right)$ .

Next let us assume the claim for  $i$ , and we consider the claim for  $i - 1$ , for  $2 \leq i \leq L - 1$ . For notational brevity, in the following, we let

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}) = \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (t)\right), \quad \mathbf {H} _ {i} (j _ {i}) = \mathbf {H} _ {i} \left(X, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (t)\right), \\ \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (c _ {i}) = \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (Z, c _ {i}; W (t)), H _ {i} (c _ {i}) = H _ {i} (X, c _ {i}; W (t)). \\ \end{array}
$$

We have:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \Delta_ {i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right) - \Delta_ {i - 1} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right)\right) \right| \\ = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sigma_ {i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}), \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}), \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}), \mathbf {H} _ {i} (j _ {i}), \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1})\right) \right. \\ - \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ \sigma_ {i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i}\right), w _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i}\right), b _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i}\right), H _ {i} \left(C _ {i}\right), H _ {i - 1} \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right)\right)\right) \right] \\ \leq Q _ {3, i} (t) + Q _ {4, i} (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

which gives

$$
G _ {i - 1} (t) \leq \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ | Q _ {3, i} (t) | + | Q _ {4, i} (t) | \right] ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2},
$$

in which we define

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {3, i} (t) = \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| \sigma_ {i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}), \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}), \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}), \mathbf {H} _ {i} (j _ {i}), \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1})\right) \right. \\ \left. - \sigma_ {i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), w _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), b _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), H _ {i} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), H _ {i - 1} \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right)\right)\right) \right|, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {4, i} \left(t\right) = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sigma_ {i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), w _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), b _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), H _ {i} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), H _ {i - 1} \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right)\right)\right) \right. \\ \left. \right. - \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ \right. \sigma_ {i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left( \right.\Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i}\right), w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i}), b _ {i} (t, C _ {i}), H _ {i} (C _ {i}), H _ {i - 1} \left(C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1})\right)) \left. \right]\left. \right|. \\ \end{array}
$$

Let us first bound  $Q_{3,i}$ . This is similar to the bounding of  $G_{L-1}$ . In particular, by Assumption 3, we have:

$$
\left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ | Q _ {3, i} (t) | \right] ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K \left(Q _ {3, i} ^ {(1)} (t) + Q _ {3, i} ^ {(2)} (t) + Q _ {3, i} ^ {(3)} (t) + Q _ {3, i} ^ {(4)} (t)\right),
$$

in which

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {3, i} ^ {(1)} (t) = \left( \right.\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + | w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) \right| + | b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) |\right)\right) \\ \times \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right) \right| \right] \Bigg) ^ {2} \Bigg) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {3, i} ^ {(2)} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} [ 1 + | \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}) | + | \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (C _ {i} (j _ {i})) |\right] \right. \\ \left. \times \left(\left| \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) - w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) - b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right|\right)\right) ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {3, i} ^ {(3)} (t) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}) \right|\right) + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right) \right|\right) \right. \right. \\ \times \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + | w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) \right| + | b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) |\right) \\ \left. \times \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right) - H _ {i} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right)\right|\right) ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {3, i} ^ {(4)} (t) = \left( \right.\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[\left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}) \right| + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right|\right)\right.\right. \\ \times \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + | w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) \right| + | b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) |\right) \\ \left. \times \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right) - H _ {i - 1} \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right)\right) \right| \right] \Bigg) ^ {2} \Bigg) ^ {1 / 2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

To bound  $Q_{3,i}^{(1)}$ , we use Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality and Fact 1 to obtain that on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {3, i} ^ {(1)} (t) \leq \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + | w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) \right| + | b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) |\right) ^ {2} \right. \\ \times \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right) \right| \right] ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K _ {T} G _ {i} (t). \\ \end{array}
$$

By Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality and Fact 1, we have a bound on  $Q_{3,i}^{(2)}$  on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {3, i} ^ {(2)} (t) \leq K \bigg (\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ 1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2} \right] \bigg) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \times \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) - w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}) - b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K _ {T} \mathscr {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

Similarly, on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {3, i} ^ {(3)} (t) \stackrel {(a)} {\leq} K _ {T} (1 + B) \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}) \right| + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right|\right) | \mathbf {H} _ {i} (j _ {i}) - H _ {i} (C _ {i} (j _ {i})) | \right] \\ \stackrel {(b)} {\leq} K _ {T} \left(1 + B\right) \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ 1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right| ^ {2} \right]\right) ^ {1 / 2} F _ {i} (t) \\ \stackrel {(c)} {\leq} K _ {T} \left(1 + B\right) F _ {i} \left(t\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where we use Fact 2 and Fact 3 in step  $(a)$ , Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality in step  $(b)$  and Fact 1 in step  $(c)$ . With the same argument, on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {3, i} ^ {(4)} (t) \stackrel {(a)} {\leq} K _ {T} (1 + B) \left( \right.\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[\left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}) \right| + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (C _ {i} (j _ {i})) \right|\right)\right.\right. \\ \left. \times \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right) - H _ {i - 1} \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right)\right) \right| \right] \Bigg) ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \stackrel {(b)} {\leq} K _ {T} \left(1 + B\right) \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ 1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(j _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right| ^ {2} \right] \right. \\ \times \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ | \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}) - H _ {i - 1} \left(C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1})\right) | ^ {2} \right] \Bigg) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \stackrel {(c)} {\leq} K _ {T} \left(1 + B\right) F _ {i - 1} (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

where again we use Fact 2 and Fact 3 in step  $(a)$ , Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality in step  $(b)$  and Fact 1 in step  $(c)$ . Therefore on the events  $\mathcal{E}$ ,  $\mathcal{E}_{t,i}^{\Delta}$  and  $\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathrm{H}}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ | Q _ {3, i} (t) | \right] ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} \left(G _ {i} (t) + \mathcal {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + (1 + B) \left(F _ {i} (t) + F _ {i - 1} (t)\right)\right) \\ \leq K _ {T} ^ {2 L - i + 2} \Bigg ((1 + B) \mathscr {D} _ {t} (W, \tilde {W}) + (1 + B ^ {2}) (\delta_ {L} ^ {\Delta} + \sum_ {j = i} ^ {L - 2} \beta_ {j}) \Bigg). \\ \end{array}
$$

Next let us bound  $Q_{4,i}$ . For brevity, let us write

$$
Z _ {i} ^ {\Delta} (t, c _ {i - 1}, c _ {i}) = \sigma_ {i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (\Delta_ {i} ^ {H} (c _ {i}), w _ {i} (t, c _ {i - 1}, c _ {i}), b _ {i} (t, c _ {i}), H _ {i} (c _ {i}), H _ {i - 1} (c _ {i - 1})).
$$

Recall that  $C_{i-1}(j_{i-1})$  and  $C_i(j_i)$  are independent. We thus have:

$$
\mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i} (j _ {i})} \left[ Z _ {i} ^ {\Delta} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right) \mid C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}) \right] = \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i}} \left[ Z _ {i} ^ {\Delta} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i}\right) \right].
$$

Furthermore  $\{C_i(j_i)\}_{j_i\in [n_i]}$  are  $\eta_{i}$ -independent by Assumption 4. We also have that almost surely,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| Z _ {i} ^ {\Delta} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right| \leq K \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right|\right) \left(1 + \left| w _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right| + \left| b _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right|\right) \\ \leq K _ {T} \left(1 + B ^ {2}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

by Assumption 3 and Fact 2. Then by Lemma 50, noting that  $\beta_{i - 1}\geq K\eta_i$

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ Q _ {4, i} (t) \right] \geq K _ {T} \left(1 + B ^ {2}\right) \beta_ {i - 1}\right) \leq \left(1 / \beta_ {i - 1}\right) \exp \left(- n _ {i} \beta_ {i - 1} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right).
$$

We thus have, by taking a union bound over  $j_{i - 1}\in [n_{i - 1}]$ , on the events  $\mathcal{E}$ ,  $\mathcal{E}_{t,i}^{\Delta}$  and  $\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathrm{H}}$ ,

$$
G _ {i - 1} (t) \leq K _ {T} ^ {2 L - i + 2} \left(\left(1 + B\right) \mathcal {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right) + \left(1 + B ^ {2}\right) \left(\delta_ {L} ^ {\Delta} + \sum_ {j = i - 1} ^ {L - 2} \beta_ {j}\right)\right)
$$

with probability at least  $1 - \left( {{n}_{i - 1}/{\beta }_{i - 1}}\right) \exp \left( {-{n}_{i}{\beta }_{i - 1}^{2}/{K}_{T}}\right)$  . We thus get:

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {t, L} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \cap \mathcal {E} _ {t, i - 1} ^ {\Delta}; \mathcal {E}\right) \geq \mathbb {P} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {t, L} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \cap \mathcal {E} _ {t, i} ^ {\Delta}; \mathcal {E}\right) - \frac {n _ {i - 1}}{\beta_ {i - 1}} \exp \left(- n _ {i - 1} \beta_ {i - 1} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right) \geq \mathbb {P} \left(\mathcal {E} _ {t, L} ^ {\mathbf {H}}; \mathcal {E}\right) - \sum_ {j = i - 1} ^ {L - 2} \frac {n _ {j}}{\beta_ {j}} \exp \left(- n _ {j + 1} \beta_ {j} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right),
$$

which proves the claim.

Step 5 - Claim 4. We reuse the notations introduced in the previous step. For  $2 \leq i \leq L$ , we have:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {b}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (t)\right) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {b} \left(Z, C _ {i} (j _ {i}); W (t)\right) \right| \\ = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sigma_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {b}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}), \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}), \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}), \mathbf {H} _ {i} (j _ {i}), \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1})\right) \right. \\ \left. - \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ \sigma_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {b}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right), w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , H _ {i} \left(C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right), H _ {i - 1} (C _ {i - 1})\right) \right] \right| \\ \leq Q _ {5, i} (t) + Q _ {6, i} (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

which gives, by Assumption 1,

$$
D _ {i} ^ {b} (t) \leq K \bigg (\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} [ | Q _ {5, i} (t) | + | Q _ {6, i} (t) | ] ^ {2} \bigg) ^ {1 / 2},
$$

in which we define

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {5, i} (t) = \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left| \sigma_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {b}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (j _ {i}), \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}), \tilde {b} _ {i} (t, j _ {i}), \mathbf {H} _ {i} (j _ {i}), \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1})\right) \right. \\ \left. - \sigma_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {b}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), w _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), b _ {i} \left(t, C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), H _ {i} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right), H _ {i - 1} \left(C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right)\right)\right) \right|, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {6, i} (t) = \left| \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sigma_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {b}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right), w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1}), C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , H _ {i} \left(C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right), H _ {i - 1} \left(C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1})\right)\right) \right. \\ \left. - \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ \sigma_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {b}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right), w _ {i} (t, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , b _ {i} (t, C _ {i} (j _ {i})) , H _ {i} \left(C _ {i} (j _ {i})\right), H _ {i - 1} (C _ {i - 1})\right) \right] \right|. \\ \end{array}
$$

Similar to the bounding of  $D_{i}^{w}(t)$ , we have on the event  $\mathcal{E}$

$$
\left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ | Q _ {5, i} (t) | \right] ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} \left(F _ {i - 1} (t) + G _ {i} (t) + \mathcal {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right) + F _ {i} (t)\right).
$$

To bound  $Q_{6,i}$ , for brevity, let us write

$$
Z _ {i} ^ {b} \left(t, c _ {i - 1}, c _ {i}\right) = \sigma_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {b}} \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(c _ {i}\right), w _ {i} \left(t, c _ {i - 1}, c _ {i}\right), b _ {i} \left(t, c _ {i}\right), H _ {i} \left(c _ {i}\right), H _ {i - 1} \left(c _ {i - 1}\right)\right).
$$

Recall that  $C_{i-1}(j_{i-1})$  and  $C_i(j_i)$  are independent. We thus have:

$$
\mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1} (j _ {i - 1})} \left[ Z _ {i} ^ {b} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \Big | C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right) \right] = \mathbb {E} _ {C _ {i - 1}} \left[ Z _ {i} ^ {b} \left(t, C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right].
$$

Furthermore  $\{C_{i - 1}(j_{i - 1})\}_{j_{i - 1}\in [n_{i - 1}]}$  are  $\eta_{i - 1}$ -independent by Assumption 4. We also have that almost surely,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| Z _ {i} ^ {b} \left(t, C _ {i - 1} \left(j _ {i - 1}\right), C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right| \leq K \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {H} \left(C _ {i} \left(j _ {i}\right)\right) \right|\right) \\ \leq K _ {T} \left(1 + B\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

by Assumption 3 and Fact 2. Then by Lemma 50, and since  $\gamma_{i}\geq K\eta_{i - 1}$

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ Q _ {6, i} \right] \geq K _ {T} (1 + B) \gamma_ {i}) \leq (1 / \gamma_ {i}) \exp \left(- n _ {i - 1} \gamma_ {i} ^ {2} / K _ {T}\right). \right.
$$

Notice that  $\delta_L^b\geq \gamma_i$ . We thus have, by taking a union bound over  $j_{i}\in [n_{i}]$ , on the events  $\mathcal{E}$ ,  $\mathcal{E}_{t,1}^{\Delta}$  and  $\mathcal{E}_{t,L}^{\mathrm{H}}$

$$
D _ {i} ^ {b} (t) \leq K _ {T} \left(\left(1 + B\right) \mathscr {D} _ {t} \left(W, \tilde {W}\right) + \left(1 + B ^ {2}\right) \delta_ {L} ^ {b}\right)
$$

with probability at least  $1 - (n_i / \gamma_i) \exp \left( -n_{i-1} \gamma_i^2 / K_T \right)$ . The claim then follows again from the union bound.

# C.1.2 Proof of Proposition 23

Proof of Proposition 23. We consider  $t \leq T$ , for a given terminal time  $T \in \epsilon \mathbb{N}_{\geq 0}$ . We again reuse the notation  $K_{t}$  from the proof of Proposition 22. Note that  $K_{t} \leq K_{T}$  for all  $t \leq \bar{T}$ . We also note that at initialization,  $\mathcal{D}_0(\mathbf{W},\tilde{W}) = 0$ . We start with a few preliminary fact:

Fact 1: moment bounds. We recall a useful fact from the proof of Proposition 22: with probability at least  $1 - KLn_{\max} \exp \left(-Kn_{\min}^{1/52}\right)$ , the event  $\mathcal{E}$  occurs, and  $\mathcal{E}$  contains the following:

$$
\| \left\| \mathbf {W} \right\| \| _ {0} = \| \tilde {W} \| \| _ {0} \leq K,
$$

$$
\max  _ {1 \leq i \leq L} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup  _ {t \leq T} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (t)\right) \right| ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}, \|   \| \tilde {W} \|   \| _ {T} \leq K _ {T}.
$$

We further remark that since  $\| \mathbf{W} \|_0 \leq K$ , from Lemma 52, we have on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\max _ {1 \leq i \leq L} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup _ {t \leq T} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor)) \right| ^ {5 0}\right) ^ {1 / 5 0}, \| \| \mathbf {W} \| \| _ {\lfloor T / \epsilon \rfloor} \leq K _ {T}.
$$

We also observe that the randomness of the event  $\mathcal{E}$  is entirely by the samples of the coupling procedure  $\{C_1(j_1),\dots,C_L(j_L):j_i\in [n_i],i = 1,\dots,L\}$ .

Fact 2: maximal bounds. We also recall another useful fact from the proof of Proposition 22: on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ , almost surely,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \max  _ {j _ {i - 1} \in [ n _ {i - 1} ], j _ {i} \in [ n _ {i} ]} \sup  _ {t \leq T} | \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | \leq K _ {T} (1 + B), \\ \max_{2\leq i\leq L}\max_{j_{i}\in [n_{i}]} \sup_{t\leq T}\left|\tilde{b}_{i}\left(t,j_{i}\right)\right|\leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

In fact, the same extends to  $\mathbf{W}$ : on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ , almost surely,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \max  _ {j _ {i - 1} \in [ n _ {i - 1} ], j _ {i} \in [ n _ {i} ]} \sup  _ {t \leq T} | \mathbf {w} _ {i} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | \leq K _ {T} (1 + B), \\ \max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \max  _ {j _ {i} \in [ n _ {i} ]} \sup  _ {t \leq T} | \mathbf {b} _ {i} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i}) | \leq K _ {T} (1 + B), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\max_{1\leq i\leq L}\max_{j_{i}\in [n_{i}]}\sup_{t\leq T}\operatorname *{ess -sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i}^{\mathbf{H}}(Z,j_{i};\mathbf{W}(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor))\right|\leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right).
$$

Indeed, let us consider the claim for  $\mathbf{w}_i$ . By Assumption 3, for  $\mathcal{P}$ -almost every  $z$ ,

$$
\sup  _ {t \geq 0} \max  _ {j _ {L - 1} \in [ n _ {L - 1} ]} | \Delta_ {L} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (z, j _ {L - 1}, 1; \mathbf {W} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor)) | \leq K \left(1 + \sup  _ {t \geq 0} \left| \Delta_ {L} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (z, 1; \mathbf {W} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor)) \right|\right) \leq K,
$$

which implies, by Assumption 1, that almost surely, for any  $j_{L - 1}\in [n_{L - 1}]$

$$
\sup  _ {t \leq T} \left| \mathbf {w} _ {L} \left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {L - 1}, 1\right) \right| \leq \operatorname {e s s} - \sup  \left| w _ {L} ^ {0} \left(C _ {L - 1}, 1\right) \right| + K T \leq K _ {T} (1 + B).
$$

Next assuming that  $\sup_{t\leq T}|\mathbf{w}_i(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor ,j_{i - 1},j_i)|\leq K_T(1 + B)$  almost surely for a given  $i\geq 2$ , by Assumption 3, we have on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ , for any  $j_{i - 1}\in [n_{i - 1}]$ ,  $t\leq T$  and  $\mathcal{P}$ -almost every  $z$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \Delta_ {i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(z, j _ {i - 1}; \mathbf {W} \left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor\right)\right) \right| \\ \leq \frac {K}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left(1 + \sup _ {t \leq T} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor)) \right|\right) (1 + | \mathbf {w} _ {i} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + | \mathbf {b} _ {i} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i}) |) \\ \leq \frac{K}{n_{i}}\sum_{j_{i} = 1}^{n_{i}}\left(1 + \sup_{t\leq T}\operatorname *{ess - sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\big|\Delta_{i}^{\mathbf{H}}\left(Z,j_{i};\mathbf{W}\left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor\right)\right)\right)\left(K_{T}\left(1 + B\right) + |\mathbf{b}_{i}\left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor ,j_{i}\right)|\right) \\ \leq K \left(1 + \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \sup  _ {t \leq T} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor)) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}\right) \left(K _ {T} (1 + B) + \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} | \mathbf {b} _ {i} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i}) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}\right) \\ \leq K _ {T} (1 + B), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last step follows from Fact 1. Again by Assumption 3, we then obtain:

$$
\left| \Delta_ {i - 1} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (z, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor)) \right| \leq K _ {T} (1 + B),
$$

which implies, by Assumption 1, that almost surely on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ , for any  $j_{i-1} \in [n_{i-1}]$  and  $j_i \in [n_i]$ :

$$
\sup  _ {t \leq T} | \mathbf {w} _ {i} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | \leq \operatorname {e s s - s u p} \left| w _ {i - 1} ^ {0} (C _ {i - 1}, C _ {i}) \right| + K _ {T} (1 + B) T \leq K _ {T} (1 + B).
$$

This proves the claim for  $\mathbf{w}_i$ , and the rest of the claims are similarly proven.

Now let us consider  $2 \leq i \leq L$  and particularly the task of bounding

$$
\left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} | \mathbf {w} _ {i} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) - \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2},
$$

which is a quantity in  $\mathcal{D}_T\left(\tilde{W},\mathbf{W}\right)$ . As shown in the proof of Proposition 22:

$$
\sup  _ {t \leq T - \zeta} \sup  _ {0 \leq \zeta^ {\prime} \leq \zeta} \max  _ {i} \left(\tilde {A} _ {i} ^ {w} (t, \zeta^ {\prime}), \tilde {A} _ {i} ^ {b} (t, \zeta^ {\prime})\right) \leq K _ {T} (1 + B) \zeta
$$

almost surely, where we recall

$$
\tilde {A} _ {i} ^ {w} (t, \zeta) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| \partial_ {1} \tilde {w} _ {i} (t + \zeta , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) - \partial_ {1} \tilde {w} _ {i} (t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2},
$$

As such, by Assumption 1, we have the decomposition:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| \mathbf {w} _ {i} \left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}\right) - \tilde {w} _ {i} \left(t, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \\ = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| \epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \xi_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (k \epsilon) \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (z (k), j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) - \int_ {s = 0} ^ {t} \partial_ {1} \tilde {w} _ {i} (s, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) d s \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| \epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \xi_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (k \epsilon) \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (z (k), j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) - \epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \partial_ {1} \tilde {w} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} + t K _ {T} (1 + B) \epsilon \\ \leq K \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| Q _ {1} \left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {2} + \left| Q _ {2} \left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} + t K _ {T} (1 + B) \epsilon , \\ \end{array}
$$

where we define

$$
Q _ {1, i} \left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}\right) = \epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} \left(Z, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)\right) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} \left(Z, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)\right) \right| \right],
$$

$$
Q _ {2, i} \left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}\right) = \left| \epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \xi_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (k \epsilon) \left(\Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (z (k), j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) - \mathbb {E} _ {Z} \left[ \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (Z, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) \right]\right) \right|.
$$

(Here  $\sum_{k=0}^{\lfloor t/\epsilon \rfloor - 1} = 0$  if  $\lfloor t/\epsilon \rfloor = 0$ .) The task is then to bound  $Q_{1,i}$  and  $Q_{2,i}$ .

Bounding  $Q_{1,i}$ . We take note of a simple identity:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{| J |} \sum_ {j \in J} \left(\epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} f (j, k)\right) ^ {2} = \epsilon^ {2} \sum_ {k _ {1} = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \sum_ {k _ {2} = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \frac {1}{| J |} \sum_ {j \in J} f (j, k _ {1}) f (j, k _ {2}) \\ \leq \epsilon^ {2} \sum_ {k _ {1} = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \sum_ {k _ {2} = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \left(\frac {1}{| J |} \sum_ {j \in J} | f (j, k _ {1}) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \left(\frac {1}{| J |} \sum_ {j \in J} | f (j, k _ {2}) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \\ = \left(\epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \left(\frac {1}{| J |} \sum_ {j \in J} | f (j, k) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}\right) ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

As such, by Assumption 3:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| Q _ {1, i} \left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq \epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} D _ {i} (k) \\ \leq K \epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \left(D _ {i} ^ {(1)} (k) + G _ {i} (k) + \mathcal {D} _ {k \epsilon} \left(\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}\right) + F _ {i} (k)\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

in which we define:

$$
D _ {i} \left(k\right) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} \left(Z, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} \left(k\right)\right) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} \left(Z, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} \left(k \epsilon\right)\right) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} D _ {i} ^ {(1)} (k) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) \right| ^ {2}\right) \right. \\ \times \frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} \left(X, j _ {i - 1}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)\right) - \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} \left(X, j _ {i - 1}; \mathbf {W} (k)\right)\right| ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
G _ {i} \left(k\right) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} \left(k \epsilon\right)\right) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} \left(k\right)\right) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2},
$$

$$
F _ {i} \left(k\right) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i} \left(X, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} \left(k \epsilon\right)\right) - \mathbf {H} _ {i} \left(X, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} \left(k\right)\right) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2}.
$$

By Lemma 52 and Fact 1, on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
D _ {i} ^ {(1)} (k) \leq K _ {T} F _ {i - 1} (k),
$$

which implies

$$
\left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} | Q _ {1, i} (\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq K _ {T} \epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \left(F _ {i - 1} (k) + G _ {i} (k) + \mathcal {D} _ {k \epsilon} \left(\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}\right) + F _ {i} (k)\right).
$$

We proceed with bounding  $F_{i}$  and  $G_{i}$ .

To bound  $F_{i}$ , by Assumption 2 and Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| F _ {i} (k) \right| ^ {2} \leq \frac {K}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left(1 + \left| \tilde {w} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \mathbf {w} _ {i} (k, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \mathbf {b} _ {i} (k, j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2}\right) \left| F _ {i - 1} (k) \right| ^ {2} \\ + K \mathcal {D} _ {k \epsilon} ^ {2} (\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}) \\ \leq K _ {T} \left| F _ {i - 1} (k) \right| ^ {2} + K \mathcal {D} _ {k \epsilon} ^ {2} \left(\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last display holds on the event  $\mathcal{E}$  by Fact 1. Notice that by Assumption 2,  $|F_{1}(k)| \leq K\mathcal{D}_{k\epsilon}(\mathbf{W},\tilde{W})$ . Therefore, on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ ,

$$
\max _ {1 \leq i \leq L} | F _ {i} (k) | \leq K _ {T} \mathcal {D} _ {k \epsilon} \left(\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}\right),
$$

which is the desired bound for  $F_{i}$ .

Next let us bound  $G_{i}$ . By Assumption 3, we have:

$$
G _ {i - 1} (k) \leq K \left(G _ {i} ^ {(1)} (k) + G _ {i} ^ {(2)} (k) + G _ {i} ^ {(3)} (k) + G _ {i} ^ {(4)} (k)\right),
$$

in which

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {i - 1} ^ {(1)} (k) = \left( \right.\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + | \mathbf {w} _ {i} (k, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i}) \right| + | \mathbf {b} _ {i} (k, j _ {i}) |\right)\right) \\ \times \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)\right) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)\right)\right| \Bigg) ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {i - 1} ^ {(2)} (k) = \left( \right.\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)) \right| + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) \right|\right)\right. \\ \left. \times \left(\left| \tilde {w} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) - \mathbf {w} _ {i} (k, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) \right| + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i}) - \mathbf {b} _ {i} (k, j _ {i}) \right|\right)\right) ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {i - 1} ^ {(3)} (k) = \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)) \right| + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) \right|\right) \right. \right. \\ \times \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + | \mathbf {w} _ {i} (k, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i}) \right| + | \mathbf {b} _ {i} (k, j _ {i}) |\right) \\ \times \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i} \left(X, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)\right) - \mathbf {H} _ {i} \left(X, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)\right)\right|) ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {i - 1} ^ {(4)} (k) = \left( \right.\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)) \right|\right. + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) \right|\right) \\ \times \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + | \mathbf {w} _ {i} (k, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i}) \right| + | \mathbf {b} _ {i} (k, j _ {i}) |\right) \\ \times \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} \left(X, j _ {i - 1}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)\right) - \mathbf {H} _ {i - 1} \left(X, j _ {i - 1}; \mathbf {W} (k)\right)\right| ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

To bound  $G_{i-1}^{(1)}$ , by Cauchy-Schwarz's inequality and Fact 1, on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {i - 1} ^ {(1)} (k) \leq \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left(1 + | \tilde {w} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + | \mathbf {w} _ {i} (k, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i}) \right| + | \mathbf {b} _ {i} (k, j _ {i}) |\right) ^ {2} \right. \\ \times \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)\right) - \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)\right)\right| \Biggr) ^ {2}\left. \right) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K _ {T} G _ {i} (k). \\ \end{array}
$$

We also have a bound on  $G_{i-1}^{(2)}$  on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {i - 1} ^ {(2)} (k) \leq K \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \underset {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} {\operatorname {e s s - s u p}} \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) \right| ^ {2}\right)\right) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \times \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} | \tilde {w} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) - \mathbf {w} _ {i} (k, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) | ^ {2} + \left| \tilde {b} _ {i} (k \epsilon , j _ {i}) - \mathbf {b} _ {i} (k, j _ {i}) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K _ {T} \mathscr {D} _ {k \epsilon} \left(\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

Similarly, by Fact 1 and Fact 2, on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
G _ {i - 1} ^ {(3)} (k) \leq K _ {T} (1 + B) \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \underset {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} {\operatorname {e s s - s u p}} \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)) \right| + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) \right|\right)
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \times \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left| \mathbf {H} _ {i} \left(X, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)\right) - \mathbf {H} _ {i} \left(X, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)\right) \right| \\ \leq K _ {T} \left(1 + B\right) \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} \left(k \epsilon\right)\right) \right| ^ {2} + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} \left(Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} \left(k\right)\right) \right| ^ {2}\right)\right) ^ {1 / 2} F _ {i} \left(k\right) \\ \leq K _ {T} (1 + B) F _ {i} (k), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {i - 1} ^ {(4)} (k) \leq K _ {T} (1 + B) \frac {1}{n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \operatorname * {e s s - s u p} _ {Z \sim \mathcal {P}} \left(1 + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \tilde {W} (k \epsilon)) \right| + \left| \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {H}} (Z, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) \right|\right) F _ {i - 1} (k) \\ \leq K _ {T} (1 + B) F _ {i - 1} (k). \\ \end{array}
$$

Therefore on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ :

$$
\begin{array}{l} G _ {i - 1} (k) \leq K _ {T} \left(G _ {i} (k) + \mathscr {D} _ {k \epsilon} (\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}) + (1 + B) \left(F _ {i} (k) + F _ {i - 1} (k)\right)\right) \\ \leq K _ {T} \left(G _ {i} (k) + (1 + B) \mathcal {D} _ {k \epsilon} (\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W})\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

Notice that by Assumption 3,

$$
G _ {L} (k) \leq K F _ {L} (k) \leq K _ {T} \mathscr {D} _ {k \epsilon} (\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}).
$$

Therefore, on the event  $\mathcal{E}$

$$
\max  _ {1 \leq i \leq L} | G _ {i} (k) | \leq K _ {T} (1 + B)   \mathcal {D} _ {k \epsilon} \left(\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}\right),
$$

which is the desired bound for  $G_{i}$ .

Together these bounds yield

$$
\max  _ {2 \leq i \leq L} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} \left| Q _ {1, i} \left(\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}\right) \right| ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \leq \epsilon K _ {T} (1 + B) \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \mathcal {D} _ {k \epsilon} (\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}).
$$

Bounding  $Q_{2,i}$ . For brevity, let us write

$$
Z _ {k} = \xi_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (k \epsilon) (\Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (z (k), j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) - \mathbb {E} _ {Z} [ \Delta_ {i} ^ {\mathbf {w}} (Z, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}; \mathbf {W} (k)) ]),
$$

$$
\underline {{Z}} _ {k} = \sum_ {\ell = 0} ^ {k - 1} Z _ {\ell}, \qquad \underline {{Z}} _ {0} = 0.
$$

Let  $\mathcal{F}_k$  be the sigma-algebra generated by  $\{z(s) : s \in \{0, \dots, k-1\}\}$ . Recall that it is independent of the samples  $\{C_1(j_1), \dots, C_L(j_L) : j_i \in [n_i], i = 1, \dots, L\}$  and hence the event  $\mathcal{E}$ . Note that  $\{\underline{Z}_k\}_{k \in \mathbb{N}}$  is a martingale adapted to  $\{\mathcal{F}_k\}_{k \in \mathbb{N}}$ . Furthermore, for  $k \leq T / \epsilon$ , the martingale difference is bounded:

$$
|Z_{k}|\leq K\operatorname *{ess -sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}|\Delta_{i}^{\mathbf{w}}\left(Z,j_{i - 1},j_{i};\mathbf{W}\left(k\right)\right)|\\ \leq K\left(1 + \operatorname *{ess -sup}_{Z\sim \mathcal{P}}\left|\Delta_{i}^{\mathbf{H}}\left(Z,j_{i};\mathbf{W}\left(k\right)\right)\right|\right)\\ \leq K_{T}\left(1 + B\right),
$$

which holds on the event  $\mathcal{E}$ , by Assumptions 1 and 3 and Fact 2. Therefore, by Theorem 43, we have:

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\max  _ {u \in \{0, 1, \dots , T / \epsilon \}} Q _ {2, i} (u, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}) \geq (1 + B) \zeta ; \mathcal {E}\right) \leq 2 \exp \left(- \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{K _ {T} T \epsilon}\right).
$$

Putting together. Applying the union bound to the bound on  $Q_{2,i}$ , we then get that on the event  $\mathcal{E}$  with probability at least  $1 - 2n_i n_{i-1} \exp \left( -\zeta^2 / (K_T T\epsilon) \right)$ , for all  $t \leq T$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sup  _ {s \leq t, j _ {i - 1} \in [ n _ {i - 1} ], j _ {i} \in [ n _ {i} ]} \left(\frac {1}{n _ {i - 1} n _ {i}} \sum_ {j _ {i - 1} = 1} ^ {n _ {i - 1}} \sum_ {j _ {i} = 1} ^ {n _ {i}} | \mathbf {w} _ {i} \left(\lfloor s / \epsilon \rfloor , j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}\right) - \tilde {w} _ {i} \left(s, j _ {i - 1}, j _ {i}\right) | ^ {2}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \\ \leq K _ {T} (1 + B) \left(\epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \mathcal {D} _ {k \epsilon} (\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}) + \zeta + \epsilon\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

One can obtain similar bounds for  $\mathbf{b}_i$  and  $\mathbf{w}_1$ . Together these bounds yield that with probability at least

$$
1 - 2 \bigg (n _ {1} + \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {L} n _ {i} n _ {i - 1} \bigg) \exp \left(- \zeta^ {2} / \left(K _ {T} T \epsilon\right)\right) - K L n _ {\max } \exp \left(- K n _ {\min } ^ {1 / 5 2}\right),
$$

we have for all  $t\leq T$

$$
\mathcal {D} _ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor \epsilon} (\tilde {W}, \mathbf {W}) \leq K _ {T} (1 + B) \left(\epsilon \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\lfloor t / \epsilon \rfloor - 1} \mathcal {D} _ {k \epsilon} (\mathbf {W}, \tilde {W}) + \zeta + \epsilon\right),
$$

which implies, by Gronwall's lemma,

$$
\mathcal {D} _ {T} (\tilde {W}, \mathbf {W}) \leq (\zeta + \epsilon) \exp (K _ {T} (1 + B)).
$$

Choosing  $\zeta = \sqrt{K_T\epsilon\log\left(2\left(n_1 + \sum_{i = 2}^{L}n_in_{i - 1}\right) / \delta\right)}$  completes the proof.

# C.1.3 Proof of Proposition 24

Proof of Proposition 24. We again reuse the notation  $K_{t}$  from the proof of Proposition 22. Note that  $K_{t} \leq K_{T}$  for all  $t \leq T$ . It is easy to see from Theorem 7 that the trajectory  $\underline{W}(t)$  exists and is unique. Let us recall the mapping  $F$  and the space  $\mathcal{W}_T$  from the proof Theorem 7; we note that  $F$  is associated with the initialization  $W(0)$ . Since  $F(\underline{W})(t) - W(0) = \underline{W}(t) - \underline{W}(0)$  and  $W$  is a fixed point of  $F$ , we have:

$$
\| W - \underline {{W}} \| _ {t} \leq \| W - \underline {{W}} \| _ {0} + \| W - F (\underline {{W}}) \| _ {t} = \| W - \underline {{W}} \| _ {0} + \| F (W) - F (\underline {{W}}) \| _ {t}.
$$

Due to truncation, it is immediate that

$$
| \underline {{w}} _ {1} (0, c _ {1}) | = | w _ {1} (0, c _ {1}) |, | \underline {{w}} _ {i} (0, c _ {i - 1}, c _ {i}) | \leq | w _ {i} (0, c _ {i - 1}, c _ {i}) |, | \underline {{b}} _ {i} (0, c _ {i}) | \leq | b _ {i} (0, c _ {i}) |, 2 \leq i \leq L.
$$

As such, by repeating the argument of Lemma 8, one can show that  $\underline{W} \in \mathcal{W}_T$  and that

$$
\mathbb {P} \left(\max  _ {T} ^ {w} (\underline {{W}}) \geq K _ {0} (T) u\right) \leq 2 L e ^ {1 - K _ {1} u ^ {2}} \quad \forall u \geq 0.
$$

Thus, Lemma 10 gives:

$$
\left\| F (W) - F (\underline {{W}}) \right\| _ {t} \leq K _ {T} \left((1 + B) \int_ {0} ^ {t} \| W - \underline {{W}} \| _ {s} d s + e ^ {- K B ^ {2}}\right),
$$

which implies, by the previous bound,

$$
